### [1] [An Information-Theoretic Perspective on Spectral Reconstruction in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10671)
*Nan Wang,Long-cheng Gui,Jian Liang,Jun Shi,Han-yang Xing*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kullback-Leibler散度的信息论框架，用于量化有限、含噪声格点关联函数对谱函数重构所提供的信息量，通过先验到后验分布的变化评估数据约束力，并结合高斯平滑与贝叶斯采样方法验证其在三峰谱及质子关联函数上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限、含噪声的格点关联函数导致谱重建存在严重病态性；需定量区分数据驱动约束与先验假设影响，以评估重建谱的可靠性。

Method: 构建基于KL散度的信息度量，分别刻画单能量点谱强度分布变化及全谱相关性约束；引入高斯加权平滑以实现能量尺度分辨；结合分层贝叶斯重建与后验采样进行数值实现。

Result: 在模拟三峰谱与近物理格点质子关联函数上，信息度量成功识别出数据强约束区域与先验主导区域，揭示了重建谱结构的可信度来源及其能量依赖性。

Conclusion: KL信息度量可作为半定量判据，有效区分数据驱动特征与先验偏差，提升格点谱函数重建的可解释性与可靠性评估能力。

Abstract: Assessing the reliability of spectral reconstruction from finite, noisy lattice correlators requires understanding the constraints supplied by the data relative to prior assumptions. We develop an information-theoretic framework using Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergences to quantify the information supplied by the data through changes from prior to posterior probability distributions. Our analysis accounts for both changes in the probability distributions of spectral strength at individual energies and constraints encoded in correlations across the spectrum. We smear these information measures over neighboring energies using Gaussian weights to obtain a smoothed assessment at a specified energy scale. We demonstrate this framework using a hierarchical Bayesian reconstruction method with posterior sampling, applied to mock correlator data generated from a three-peak spectrum and a near-physical lattice proton correlator. These calculations show that the information measures help explain the properties of reconstructed spectra. They also provide a semiquantitative criterion for assessing how strongly reconstructed spectral structures are constrained by the data and how reliable they are in light of prior effects.

</details>


### [2] [Constrained field-space estimators for background-field lattice QCD observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10773)
*Scott Moerschbacher,Frank X. Lee*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文比较了固定阶数的磁场响应系数提取方法（如O(B^4)–O(B^8) stencil）与协方差优化的广义最小二乘估计器在格点QCD弱场响应分析中的统计精度与模型鲁棒性，发现固定stencil在精度上几乎无损且对高阶响应项遗漏更稳健，而协方差优化虽小幅提升精度（1–2%），却显著放大模型误设风险（如违反Δ重子磁矩严格比值），凸显统计精度与物理可靠性之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估格点QCD中弱磁场响应系数（如磁矩）提取方法的统计效率与模型鲁棒性，尤其关注固定有限阶数stencil与协方差加权线性估计在面对高阶响应项遗漏时的表现差异。

Method: 将磁场响应系数提取建模为磁场强度B²空间中的线性估计问题；对比固定阶数差分模板（O(B⁴)–O(B⁸)）与基于时间相关协方差矩阵的广义最小二乘（GLS）估计器；利用淬火系综上的核子双比关联函数及满足解析约束的重子十重态（decuplet）关联函数作为控制样本；引入零空间检验（null-space test）量化模型误设敏感性。

Result: 固定O(B⁴)–O(B⁸) stencil在精度上与协方差优化GLS相当（损失可忽略），且对高阶响应遗漏高度稳健；GLS虽在轻夸克质量区提升1–2%精度（两态时间拟合下达3–10%），但其权重对遗漏响应模式具有3–4个数量级更高的杠杆效应，导致磁矩估计偏移达1.8个边缘标准误差；decuplet控制实验显示GLS严重违反Δ⁺/Δ⁺⁺磁矩比=1/2的精确对称性（偏差10–25σ），而固定stencil在生产窗口内与之兼容。

Conclusion: 固定阶数stencil在格点弱场响应分析中提供了更优的精度-鲁棒性平衡；协方差优化虽提升统计效率，但以牺牲模型可靠性为代价；结果强调：高统计精度不等价于物理可信度，尤其在存在未建模响应模式时。

Abstract: Background-field lattice calculations extract weak-field response coefficients from correlators computed at several field strengths. We treat this extraction as linear estimation in field space and compare fixed cancellation stencils with covariance-optimized, generalized-least-squares estimators of the same coefficient, using archival nucleon double ratios on about 1000 quenched configurations at six quark masses and decuplet correlators for which exact answers follow from the lattice contractions. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils are nearly coincident and cost almost nothing in precision. Weight-free null-space tests reject the two-term response model in most nucleon ensembles; whether they reject the three-term model depends on the temporal fits and on the noise of the estimated temporal covariance, which raises the null-contrast error by a factor of 1.7-2.4. With that noise included, covariance optimization gains 1-2% in precision at the five lighter masses (3-30% with two-state time fits, which also lower moments by 3-10%) and nothing on independent data halves, while its weights carry three to four orders of magnitude more leverage on omitted response modes than the fixed stencils and move the moments by up to 1.8 marginal standard errors. The decuplet controls show what such leverage does under misspecification: the first-order responses obey $\mu_{\Delta^+}/\mu_{\Delta^{++}}=1/2$ and $\mu_{\Delta^0}=0$ exactly. The lowest-order estimate misses the first by 10-25 standard errors. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils satisfy it within errors in the production windows, whereas the correlated linear fit in $B^2$, with the same constraints as the $O(B^4)$ stencil, misses it by 3-17%; the null-space test rejects that fit's model. The results distinguish statistical precision from robustness to omitted response modes; they are not physical-point magnetic moments.

</details>


### [3] [Flavor decomposition of the nucleon axial and tensor charges from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10816)
*C. Alexandrou,S. Bacchio,M. Di Carlo,A. Evangelista,C. Iona,F. Pittler,G. Spanoudes*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用四种不同格距的twisted-mass clover-improved费米子组态，计算了核子的轴矢荷与张量荷，包括连通与非连通贡献，并给出其味分解结果；结果与实验值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定核子的轴矢荷g_A和张量荷g_T对检验标准模型、理解强子自旋结构及约束超越标准模型的新物理至关重要；需控制格点QCD中离散化误差、有限体积效应与夸克质量外推等系统误差。

Method: 采用四组具有近似相同物理体积（~5.5 fm）但不同格距（a = 0.05–0.08 fm）的twisted-mass + clover改善费米子组态；u/d、s、c夸克质量调至接近物理值；完整计算轴矢流与张量流在核子态上的矩阵元，包含连通图与非连通图贡献，以提取isovector（u−d）与isoscalar（u+d）荷。

Result: g_A^{u−d} = 1.263(34)(26)，与实验值一致；g_A^{u+d} = 0.458(16)(1)；g_T^{u−d} = 0.951(21)(5)；g_T^{u+d} = 0.556(16)(5)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在多格距、物理夸克质量、可控系统误差下给出了高精度的核子轴矢与张量荷，验证了格点QCD对基本守恒流矩阵元的可靠预言能力，为轻子-核子深度非弹性散射及中微子核反应提供关键输入。

Abstract: We determine the nucleon axial and tensor charges using four ensembles of twisted-mass clover-improved fermions with approximately equal physical volumes of about 5.5~fm and four lattice spacings, $ a=0.05$~fm, 0.06~fm, 0.07~fm, and 0.08~fm. The mass of the degenerate up and down, strange and charm quarks are tuned to approximately their physical values. We compute both connected and disconnected contributions to determine the isovector and isoscalar charges and their flavour decomposition. For the nucleon isovector axial charge we find $g_A^{u-d}=1.263(34)(26)$, in agreement with the experimental value. For the isoscalar axial charge we find $g_A^{u+d}=0.458(16)(1)$, while for the corresponding tensor charges we find $g_T^{u-d}=0.951(21)(5)$ and $g_T^{u+d}=0.556(16)(5)$.

</details>


### [4] [Evaluating generative samplers for lattice field theory by scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11108)
*Satoshi Iso,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种尺度分辨的诊断方法，用于评估格点场论中生成式采样器对长程观测量的准确性；该方法基于粗粒化模型与目标分布之间的重叠，并揭示了全局有效样本量（ESS）不足以反映长波长物理量的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用全局有效样本量（ESS）评估生成式采样器性能，但该指标易受短程模式误差主导，无法可靠反映长距离观测量（如磁化率、关联函数）的准确性。

Method: 引入基于粗粒化分布重叠（coarse-grained overlap）的尺度分辨诊断；在格点截断尺度处该重叠决定渐近ESS分数，降低截断尺度可分离并识别长波长误差；结合路径权重ESS、边际Metropolis检验、精确局域更新及自由场理论分析进行验证。

Result: 在自由标量场理论中，两个ESS相近的采样器对磁化率分别产生32%偏差和零偏差；在二维ϕ⁴临界区域，扩散采样器显示路径权重ESS分数随体积迅速下降，但低阶磁化矩仍接近目标值；边际Metropolis检验配合局域更新可显著减小残余偏差，但有限弛豫长度下仍有剩余偏差。

Conclusion: 全局ESS不是长距离物理量准确性的充分判据；尺度分辨重叠诊断能更可靠地刻画不同动量尺度上的采样质量；需独立获取或计算目标边际分布以避免权重估计带来的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Generative samplers for lattice field theory are commonly evaluated by their effective sample size (ESS). We show that a small global ESS need not imply inaccurate long-distance observables: errors spread over many short-distance modes can dominate this diagnostic. We introduce a scale-resolved diagnostic based on the overlap between coarse-grained model and target distributions. At the lattice cutoff, this overlap determines the asymptotic ESS fraction; lower cutoffs isolate long-wavelength errors. In the exactly solvable free theory, two samplers with comparable ESS yield a $32\%$ bias and no bias in the susceptibility, respectively. In two-dimensional $\phi^4$ theory near criticality, trained diffusion samplers show the same separation of scales: their path-weight ESS fraction falls rapidly over the volumes studied, while low moments of the magnetization remain close to their target values. Varying the reference parameters substantially changes infrared observables, with little effect on the log-weight variance. Given the target magnetization distribution, a marginal Metropolis test maintains an acceptance rate of order one in our tests. Combined with exact local updates, it reduces the remaining mismatch, although residual bias remains at finite relaxation length. In the free field, uniformly bounded relative errors in the proposal mode variances give a volume-independent bound on the number of updates needed for a prescribed accuracy of the chain's stationary mode variances at fixed correlation length. The target marginal must be supplied or computed separately; estimating it from the samplers' own weights can inherit the poor global overlap.

</details>


### [5] [Renormalized symmetry probes for emergent symmetries and non-chiral lattice fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12142)
*Ting-Wai Chiu*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种RG不变的对称性恢复探针方法，用于高温度QCD中通过重归一化介子关联函数比值κ_AB来探测手征-自旋等对称性的恢复，特别适用于格点规范理论中手征对称性破缺或近似对称的情形（如涌现的手征-自旋SU(2)_CS对称性），并在N_f=2+1+1物理点域墙费米子模拟中验证了轻夸克到粲夸克扇区的手征对称性分层恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高温QCD中，对称性恢复表现为不同对称通道间强子关联函数的简并；但当格点正则化破坏手征对称性，或对称性本身仅为低能涌现（如SU(2)_{CS}）时，需构造RG不变、方案无关且能消除截断依赖的可观测量。

Method: 引入参考扇区（\bar ud）对各味扇区热/屏蔽介子关联函数进行通道匹配重归一化，定义对称性比值κ_AB = C_A / C_B 与重归一化因子比R_AB；利用R_AB吸收正则化依赖（如Wilson/twisted-mass/staggered费米子中的通道特异性Z因子），使κ_AB在精确对称下严格RG不变，在涌现对称下仅保留物理尺度依赖；对自由夸克解析计算κ_{fade}=2/3、κ_{CS}≤1/3作为基准。

Result: 在N_f=2+1+1物理点域墙费米子模拟（a=0.064 fm, T=192–1539 MeV）中，确认手征对称性从轻夸克扇区向粲夸克扇区分层恢复，该结论在重归一化后保持不变；\bar uc和\bar sc扇区展现出最宽的chiral-spin窗口及更紧阈值，表明含单粲夸克的自旋-1介子是最优chiral-spin探针通道。

Conclusion: 所提κ_AB框架为格点QCD中涌现对称性（特别是SU(2)_{CS}）的定量探测提供了首个严格RG不变且正则化鲁棒的方案；分层恢复现象是物理的、非人工的，并指向粲夸克介子作为chiral-spin物理的理想实验室。

Abstract: The degeneracy of hadron correlators in symmetry-related channels signals symmetry restoration in high-temperature QCD. RG-invariant probes of this degeneracy need care when the regularization breaks chiral symmetry, or when the symmetry is not exact in the massless action, as for the emergent chiral-spin symmetry $SU(2)_{CS}$. We renormalize the thermal and screening meson correlators of every flavor sector by the thermal correlator of the same channel in the $\bar ud$ sector at a common reference point. The symmetry ratio $\kappa_{AB}$ then depends on the renormalization only through the ratio $R_{AB}$. For exact symmetries, $R_{AB}=1$ and $\kappa_{AB}$ is RG invariant and scheme independent in mass-independent schemes. For emergent symmetries, $R_{AB}\neq1$ carries the relative logarithmic cutoff dependence of the two channels, which cancels in $\kappa_{AB}$, and the residual dependence on the reference scale is physical. The framework applies to Wilson, twisted-mass and staggered fermions, where $R_{AB}$ absorbs the channel-dependent renormalization, and the tensor pair $(T_k,X_k)$ keeps $R_{T_kX_k}=1$ on isotropic Wilson-type lattices. Lattice artifacts are a small correction to the chiral-spin probes $\kappa_{CS}$ and $\kappa_{fade}$, but as large as the signal of the exact-symmetry probes. For free quarks, we derive $\kappa_{fade}=2/3$ for every mass and separation, and $\kappa_{CS}\le1/3$. We apply the renormalized probes to $N_f=2+1+1$ QCD with optimal domain-wall quarks at the physical point, with $a=0.064$ fm and $T=192$-$1539$ MeV. The hierarchical restoration of the chiral symmetries from the light to the charm sectors is confirmed, and is invariant under renormalization. The $\bar uc$ and $\bar sc$ sectors have the widest chiral-spin windows, also at a tighter threshold. Thus spin-1 mesons with one charm quark are the most attractive channels for the chiral-spin regime.

</details>


### [6] [Optimised Distillation for Nucleon Correlation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12474)
*J. A. Crawford,J. Finkenrath,L. Barca*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出通过调节拉普拉斯算子本征模对关联函数的贡献，构建优化的蒸馏轮廓（distillation profiles），以增强核子基态的重叠并抑制激发态污染，从而改善两、三点关联函数中基态能量的提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子关联函数的计算与分析对理解核子能谱和结构至关重要，但面临早期欧氏时间激发态主导和晚期欧氏时间信噪比指数衰减的双重挑战，导致难以确定可靠的基态能量提取时间窗。

Method: 采用基于拉普拉斯本征模的蒸馏方法，设计可调制各本征模权重的优化蒸馏轮廓，构造具有高基态重叠、低激发态污染的核子插值算符。

Result: 实现了针对核子的优化蒸馏轮廓构造，并在两、三点关联函数中验证了其对激发态的显著压制效果。

Conclusion: 优化蒸馏轮廓是提升核子基态能量及矩阵元精度提取的有效手段，为格点QCD中强子谱与结构研究提供了更稳健的算符构造框架。

Abstract: The calculation and analysis of nucleon correlation functions is essential for understanding the observed spectra and structure of these ubiquitous hadrons. Two challenges arise in their analysis: at early Euclidean times, they are dominated by excited state contributions and at later Euclidean times, the signal-to-noise ratio degrades exponentially. This leads to a common challenge in identifying a reasonable window on which to extract the ground state energy. By modulating the contribution of each Laplacian eigenmode to the correlator via distillation profiles, we build optimised operators which improve the overlap with the nucleon ground state. In these proceedings, we present our progress on constructing optimised distillation profiles for nucleons and their excited state suppression in two- and three-point correlation functions.

</details>

Method: 结合量纲分析与哈密顿形式下的连续对称性（如空间旋转、平移、规范不变性），系统构造Symanzik有效哈密顿量；利用海森堡运动方程、约束条件及算符恒等式进行算符约化。

Result: 给出了ϕ⁴标量理论与纯规范理论中至O(a²)（a为空间格距）的所有独立有效算符；验证了经典极限下与已有拉格朗日形式改进结果一致；确立了哈密顿格点改进的普适操作流程。

Conclusion: 该框架为哈密顿格点QFT提供了严格、对称性保持且可系统外推至高阶的改进路径，是迈向非微扰哈密顿重整化与连续极限精确研究的关键步骤。

Abstract: We propose a framework to construct the Symanzik effective Hamiltonian for lattice quantum field theories directly based on dimensional analysis and symmetries in the Hamiltonian formulation. This allows to classify all operators which can contribute at a given order in the spatial lattice spacing. This list can be reduced using the equations of motion and other identities. We exemplify the framework for $\phi^4$ and pure gauge theory, obtaining results consistent with earlier publications for the case of classical improvement. It provides the means to develop a non-perturbative improvement programme in the Hamiltonian formulation of lattice quantum field theories in the future.

</details>


### [8] [Unpolarized proton PDF with physical quark masses and large momenta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12730)
*Xiang Gao,Andrew Hanlon,Jinchen He,Xiangyu Jiang,Swagato Mukherjee,Peter Petreczky,Qi Shi,Yong Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）在物理夸克质量下，基于大动量有效理论（LaMET）框架与库仑规范固定算符，首次高精度计算了质子的同位旋矢量（u−d）无极化部分子分布函数（PDF），结果与唯象PDF在x ∈ [0.25, 0.75]区间内一致，并在坐标空间虚部实现首次吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统格点QCD无法直接计算光锥PDF的困难，通过LaMET框架将大动量下的准PDF（quasi-PDF）匹配至物理PDF，实现第一性原理对核子结构的定量检验。

Method: 采用(2+1)-味格点系综（a = 0.076 fm），结合库仑规范固定的夸克双线性算符、运动学增强的核子插值算符及高统计精度测量；使用静止RI/MOM与混合方案进行无规整化依赖的重整化；通过NLO微扰匹配加NLL重求和，将准PDF转换为MS-bar scheme下的光锥PDF。

Abstract: We present a lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) calculation of the proton isovector ($u-d$) unpolarized parton distribution function (PDF) at physical sea and valence quark masses, using Coulomb-gauge-fixed quark bilinears within the large-momentum effective theory framework. We use the static regularization-independent momentum subtraction and hybrid schemes to renormalize the bare matrix elements. This agreement is further supported by a comparison in coordinate space, where our short-distance lattice matrix elements are consistent with those obtained from the phenomenological PDF through NLO matching. In particular, we observe close agreement in the imaginary part for the first time. Together, these results represent an encouraging step toward precision benchmarking of lattice QCD calculations of PDFs.

Conclusion: RIAIC为带相关误差和协方差正则化的高能物理数据分析提供了鲁棒、普适的模型比较与子集选择工具，拓展了信息论准则在强关联数据拟合中的适用范围。

Abstract: We generalize the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) to allow model and data subset selection when regularized fits are performed on correlated data. While the standard generalization to regularized fits is inherently dependent on the type of regularization applied to the data covariance matrix, our definition, the RIAIC, is, on average, regularization-independent. The regularization-independence is achieved by defining the new Information Criterion as an estimator of the model-dependent contribution to the correlated Kullback--Leibler divergence. The Bayesian extension is derived as well. We test the new criterion on lattice QCD data by performing regularized fits and show that it reproduces the selection properties of the standard correlated AIC, with fluctuations which are compatible with the expected statistical uncertainties. These results enable the application of subset and model selection techniques to cases where regularizations are applied to the covariance matrix of the fitted dataset.

</details>

The Belle II analyses use the Run~1 data sample of $428\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, supplemented by $980\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of Belle data for the $K_S^0K_S^0$ measurements. We report preliminary results for the $\eta h^+$ modes, complementary tests of the isospin structure of $D\to\pi\pi$, and an application of inclusive charm-flavor tagging. All reported asymmetries are consistent with $C\!P$ symmetry within the present precision.

Abstract: The precise measurement of neutrino properties is among the highest priorities in fundamental particle physics, involving many experimental and theoretical efforts worldwide. Since the experiments rely on the interactions of neutrinos in the nuclear environment, the planned advances in the scope and precision of these experiments require a commensurate effort in understanding and modeling the hadronic and nuclear physics of these interactions, as incorporated in neutrino event generators. This modeling is essential to every phase of the experimental analyses, and the theoretical uncertainties play an important role in interpreting experimental results. In this Review of current challenges, we summarize the open questions in neutrino-nucleus interactions at GeV-scale energies, and discuss in detail the potential impacts of these unknowns on the measurement of neutrino properties, using the determination of oscillation parameters as a central example. After an Executive Summary and a concise Overview of the issues, we address how the neutrino event generators treat these challenges, how electron-nucleus interactions can help understanding these challenges and the important unanswered questions for each underlying physics process---from quasi-elastic to deep-inelastic scattering. Across every topic, we find that current challenges can be met only through active support and collaboration among specialists in strong interactions and electroweak physics, including theorists and experimentalists from both the nuclear and high-energy physics communities.

Abstract: Atmospheric neutrinos span a broad range of energies and baselines, offering a complementary probe of leptonic CP violation. However, a JUNO-like liquid scintillator detector without a magnetic field cannot directly separate neutrino and antineutrino interactions, reducing CP violation sensitivity. This study investigates whether machine learning based statistical separation can improve CP violation sensitivity in a 20-kton detector. $\nu_{\mu}$ and $\nu_e$ charged current events are generated with GENIE (using Honda flux) in a JUNO-like detector geometry and are reweighted using OscProb three flavor oscillation probabilities incorporating Earth matter effects and NuFIT parameters. Detector response is approximated by smearing lepton and hadronic energies, neutrino direction, and neutron multiplicity. Logistic regression, XGBoost, and multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifiers are trained on inelasticity, neutron multiplicity, and charged lepton energy, with XGBoost selected for the analysis. CP violation sensitivity is evaluated for a 200 kton-year exposure using a Poisson likelihood with systematic nuisance parameters and marginalization over five oscillation parameters within their respective $3\sigma$ ranges. XGBoost achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of approximately 0.83 for both flavor samples. Under the square root approximation, peak CP violation sensitivity improves from 1.49 $\sigma$ to 1.96 $\sigma$ for normal mass ordering and from 1.14 $\sigma$ to 1.61 $\sigma$ for inverted mass ordering. Machine learning based statistical neutrino-antineutrino separation can recover part of the CP violation sensitivity lost in charge blind detectors. These preliminary results motivate further studies incorporating validated detector response and systematic uncertainties.

Abstract: Beam-beam electromagnetic interactions affect the luminosity, collision energy, polarization, and particle backgrounds relevant to precision measurements at electron-positron colliders. We investigate these effects using Fast $e^{\pm}e^{\pm}$ Beam and Luminosity (FeeBL), a new beam-beam simulation framework, and compare its predictions with published results from LEP and proposed future colliders. We reproduce the analytical field-integration calculations of LEP Bhabha scattering and identify an approximately 2% bias in previously published beam-deflection simulations. Applying the corresponding luminosity correction and a revised uncertainty assessment motivated by the observed agreement among the four LEP experiments yields ($N_\nu = 2.9965 \pm 0.0040$), compared with the previously reported ($2.9963 \pm 0.0074$). The revised uncertainty is conditional on an assumed common rescaling of experiment specific uncertainties and motivates further investigation of the original uncertainty budgets. We identify and correct implementation errors in the modeling of Breit-Wheeler, Bethe-Heitler, and Landau-Lifshitz pair production, with implications for detector backgrounds at Higgs factories. Simulated beam depolarization and luminosity spectra are compared with existing ILC and HALHF predictions. Finally, we investigate collision energy monochromatization for direct Higgs production, obtaining sub-MeV collision energy peak widths in exploratory linear collider configurations at substantial luminosity cost, while finding significantly weaker narrowing for the FCC-ee configurations studied. We derive a rigid Gaussian beam overlap model and estimate that FCC-ee needs roughly 10 m of horizontal only dispersion to reach widths of $\approx1~\mathrm{MeV}$.

Abstract: Dark photons are hypothetical massive vector bosons that couple to ordinary photons through kinetic mixing $\chi$. Dark photon dark matter (DPDM) converts into photons at a metal surface, with a frequency set by its mass. Using cryogenic K- and Ka-band receivers with a broadband spectrometer, we searched for this signal over 74--159 $\mu$eV/$c^2$ (18--38.5 GHz) with an integration time of $4.8\times10^5$ s per frequency bin. We found no statistically significant signal and set upper limits of $\chi<(3.1$--$17)\times10^{-12}$ at the 95% confidence level over 99.8% of the search range, assuming DPDM constitutes all local dark matter. These results improve on previous limits by more than an order of magnitude over most of 74--110 $\mu$eV/$c^2$ and provide the first direct limits over most of 110--159 $\mu$eV/$c^2$, approximately two orders of magnitude below cosmological bounds.

Abstract: In this Letter, we develop a graph-geometric framework and prove that the pentagonal bipyramid (PBP) is the unique global minimiser of both the seven-particle Lennard-Jones energy in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and the seven-charge Thomson energy on $S^2$, up to rigid symmetries and particle relabelling. For the Thomson problem, this strengthens a recent deterministic global-optimality certificate to numerical tolerance into an exact global-optimality and uniqueness theorem. A weak-interaction graph, defined by thresholding pair geometry, organises configuration space without imposing symmetry or discarding any pair interaction. For Lennard-Jones, pair-energy counting, graph classification, subset bounds, and force balance reduce all possible minimisers to 29 bounded domains; exact geometric certificates exclude 28, localise the survivor near the PBP, and a uniform restoring-energy estimate proves uniqueness. The Thomson proof follows the same progression: graph reduction, geometric localisation, and rigidity, but finalises through whole-domain coercivity and a global localisation theorem. The same fivefold order therefore emerges in two very different physical settings: an unconstrained cluster with a preferred bond length and purely repelling charges confined to a sphere, showing a common geometric organising principle for seven particles that is not tied to either interaction.

Result: 1) 正性提升 ⇔ 自对偶 ⇔ 量子Markov–Dobrushin–Doeblin小化成立；2) 等价于Kraus空间的HS正交补不含秩一算子；3) Kraus秩 ≥ 2d−1，且该界可达；4) qubit/qutrit情形存在有效判定算法；5) 无限维中正性提升不保证Lindblad–Gorini–Kossakowski–Sudarshan范数收缩。

Abstract: A Markov operator on a matrix algebra, that is, a unital completely positive map, is called positivity improving if it maps every nonzero positive element to an invertible one. This is the noncommutative counterpart of a stochastic matrix with strictly positive entries. We show that positivity improvement is self-dual and equivalent to a faithful quantum Markov--Dobrushin--Doeblin minorization. We also compare the scalar trace minorization with the optimal common-lower-bound construction of the quantum Markov--Dobrushin coefficient. We then characterize positivity improvement through the Kraus space of the map: a Markov operator is positivity improving if and only if the Hilbert--Schmidt orthogonal complement of its Kraus space contains no operator of rank one. As a consequence the Kraus rank of a positivity improving Markov operator on the algebra of $d\times d$ matrices is at least $2d-1$, and an explicit Hankel-type channel shows that this bound is attained in every dimension. Although deciding positivity improvement is NP-hard in general, we obtain complete and effectively checkable classifications for qubits and qutrits, together with families of examples (Pauli, Weyl and Werner--Holevo channels) showing that all our estimates are optimal. Finally we show that, in infinite dimensions, positivity improvement no longer implies any contraction of the Markov operator.

</details>

He was awarded the Einstein Medal in 2015 for important contributions to general relativity, in particular the development of the ADM formalism. Abstract: Local anomaly polynomials describe local variations, but a normalized Green-Schwarz phase also depends on global background data. We explain this dependence for ten-dimensional three-sphere bundles of quaternionic lines over closed spin seven-manifolds. The backgrounds retain two specified $\mathrm{Spin}(16)$ gauge bundles and an integral differential trivialization on the base, including its boundary identifications. The trivialized class is the sum of half the first Pontryagin class of the tangent bundle and the second Chern class of the quaternionic line's underlying complex bundle. For these backgrounds, the calculation combines a generally nonzero local Green-Schwarz integral with a real eta invariant evaluated by a twelve-dimensional Atiyah-Patodi-Singer filling. Together, these terms determine an integral relative characteristic number; the additive response is one third of that number, modulo integers. When the base's third and fourth rational cohomology groups vanish, the response equals eight times the Crowley-Goette invariant, without assuming that the base is simply connected. A calibration using the actual quaternionic line bundle gives additive value one third on $\mathrm{Sp}(2)$, recovering the known third-root phase. Distinct integral String data on one fixed ten-manifold give all three phase values, showing which global background information the response can detect. The physical identifications retain their prescribed fermion data, while the separate application to integral topological modular forms and vertex operator superalgebras requires compatible integral lifts. The response calculation constructs neither a microscopic realization nor an equivalence of complete theories.

</details>

Abstract: The Einstein equations constrain the transverse Taylor series at a degenerate Killing horizon, but even complete knowledge of that series need not determine a smooth spacetime: a nonzero difference can vanish to every order at the horizon. We show that this ambiguity disappears when the limiting metric on the space of Killing orbits admits a spacelike graph over a compact horizon section. In dimensions $d\geq4$, smooth vacuum Einstein germs with the same marked transverse jets are then locally isometric on a two-sided neighborhood of the whole section. The proof combines a terminal wave-map gauge with energy estimates on the orbit cylinder and yields a finite weighted matching criterion. For unwarped horizons with positive-Einstein sections, a condition on the full Lichnerowicz spectrum reduces the smooth classification to parallel first transverse data. All such data have convergent multiwarped Einstein realizations, including examples whose sections have negative sectional-curvature directions. We also obtain a finite-regularity version for round sections. The continuation argument extends to four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory and gives smooth charged-Nariai rigidity. Finally, rotating Nariai data admit the required spacelike graph throughout the regular branch; an all-order tensor estimate yields smooth Kerr--de Sitter or rotating-Nariai rigidity for $a^2/r_0^2\leq1/8$, without assuming ambient axial symmetry.

Abstract: Direct-product spacetimes offer a tractable route to exact solutions in gravity theories beyond general relativity. We classify the resulting solution spaces and determine how the allowed geometries depend on the gravitational theory and, in quadratic gravity, on the curvature couplings. Relations governing metrics with only constant-curvature $m$-dimensional subspaces are purely algebraic. We further examine conformal relations to linear-gravity solutions, generalized Petrov types, and compatibility with electromagnetic sources. Particularly noteworthy is that standard static black-hole geometries are absent from the direct-product sector of the considered form of conformal gravity for $n>4$. Four- and six-dimensional cases are analyzed in detail, and explicit analytic solutions illustrating the resulting structure are presented whenever possible.

The tidal deformabilities are consistent with multimessenger constraints from GW170817 and NICER. In addition, the theoretical mass-compactness relation and its prediction intervals show good agreement with observational estimates. Finally, Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis provides statistically reliable constraints on the tidal Love number, compactness, and perturbation parameter. These results establish a physically viable and observationally compatible framework for describing anisotropic neutron stars within General Relativity.

Abstract: We investigate the shadow and optical appearance of the Bardeen black hole (BH) within nonlinear electrodynamics (NLED). Since photon propagation in nonlinear electromagnetic field can be equivalently described as null-geodesic motion in the effective metric induced by NLED, we derive the corresponding effective optical geometry of the NLED-Bardeen BH. Furthermore, by examining the horizon-photon sphere-shadow inequality, we demonstrate that the NLED-Bardeen BH does not satisfy the complete inequality chain. In addition, we investigate photon dynamics and the corresponding optical images generated by both thin disk and spherical accretion models. Increasing $g/M$ reduces the photon sphere radius and critical impact parameter, resulting in a more compact shadow and an inward shift of the emission peak. For the thin disk accretion model, photon trajectories are classified into direct emission, lensing ring, and photon ring components according to the corresponding impact parameter. We find that Model 1 exhibits three distinct intensity peaks, whereas Models 2 and 3 display two and one peaks. The systematic dependence of these features on the magnetic charge indicates that the NLED-Bardeen BH may exhibit observational signatures distinguishable from those of the Bardeen BH. In static spherical accretion, the intensity peaks near the critical impact parameter because of the enhanced optical path length, while infalling spherical accretion introduces an additional Doppler redshift that suppresses the observed intensity. These results indicate that the shadow size, ring structure, and intensity distribution can serve as potential observational signatures of NLED effects in the strong-field regime.

Abstract: We focus on the subcritical case in Analogue Gravity for a subluminal dispersion relation, and investigate the problem of pair creation in the absence of an horizon analytically, for the subcritical white hole case. We limit our attention to weakly dispersive media. In particular, we take into account for simplicity the Corley model and adopt an approach inspired by quantum mechanics in the so-called overbarrier scattering. The presence of complex turning points is shown to play a fundamental role in allowing a mode conversion which would be absent in a naive WKB approach. We show that there is still a pair creation phenomenon, which, in the subcritical case, is non-Planckian and without a Hawking partner. Our fully analytical results are qualitatively in agreement with the literature at hand. Furthermore we confirm the existence of a critical regime betwen the subcritical and the transcritical ones where no pair creation occurs.

</details>

Abstract: Third-generation gravitational-wave observatories will observe binary neutron-star coalescences for hours before merger. Over these extended time windows, Earth's motion and the long signal durations make standard search methods computationally demanding for low-latency detection. We propose an approach based on short-time Fourier transforms and convolutional neural networks that implicitly marginalizes over extrinsic parameters, effectively reducing the search to the chirp mass. The network learns the signals' nonlinear time-frequency morphology, approximating the optimal Bayesian detection statistic. Using a large-scale campaign of simulated signal injections, we compare the network's performance with a GPU-optimized matched-filtering baseline for both the ET$\Delta$ and ET2L configurations of the Einstein Telescope and signal durations from approximately 25 minutes ($f_{\rm min} = 5$ Hz) to approximately 6 hours ($f_{\rm min} = 2$ Hz). The network achieves comparable detection sensitivity while reducing computational costs by over four orders of magnitude, bringing processing times from minutes to milliseconds. This efficiency paves the way for rapid early-warning alerts for multi-messenger astronomy in the third-generation detector era.

Abstract: We reconstruct homogeneous stealth scalar fields on prescribed Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker backgrounds with constant or evolving dark-energy equations of state. The scalar stress tensor vanishes, so the expansion history is supported by an independent background sector. Eliminating the self-interaction potential yields a Riccati equation for the scalar profile. We use it to distinguish exact stealthness from global regularity and from the stronger identification of a term in the scalar stress tensor with the prescribed dark-energy density. We prove that this sector-preserving identification is not dynamically invariant in a mixed flat constant-$w$ cosmology. For radiation-free $w$CDM backgrounds, a hypergeometric representation allows the regular and singular branches to be separated by a background-dependent threshold. At conformal coupling the reconstruction instead reduces to an exact comoving-distance quadrature, valid for arbitrary regular flat histories, including radiation. A CPL phantom-divide crossing does not singularize the background stealth equation, although the perturbations of the prescribed dark-energy sector require an independent treatment. The scalar--tensor description gives luminal high-frequency propagation under a positive effective Planck mass. If the field tracks its cosmological value locally without screening, Cassini constraints strongly suppress the corresponding late-time gravitational responses.

Abstract: We present an approach for numerical simulations of pure radiation fluids within the general relativistic hydrodynamics framework. The approach models radiation as a self-gravitating perfect fluid with vanishing rest mass, enabling simulations of radiation fluids in strong-gravity spacetimes. Building on the existing relativistic hydrodynamics code Whisky, within the Einstein Toolkit framework, we introduce modifications to incorporate radiation fluids, including updates to the source terms, recovery scheme, and Riemann solvers. Modifications are validated using standard shock tube tests. We present results for radiation accretion onto a single black hole, as well as a photon star solution for radiation in unstable hydrostatic equilibrium, demonstrating both collapse and dispersion under perturbations.

Abstract: Rotating black holes can amplify waves that scatter off them, a process known as superradiance that fluid analogues bring into the laboratory. Separating the horizon's role from dissipation in the surrounding fluid remains difficult. Here we realise a rotating analogue black hole in a lattice fluid: a shallow-water vortex whose horizon is set by a topographic sill, so that the only sink lies behind a supercritical layer that lets nothing out. We measure superradiance as energy extraction: the flux at the horizon is outward for co-rotating waves whose pattern rotates more slowly than the horizon, and inward for faster waves, counter-rotating waves and a non-rotating hole. A scan over three lattice viscosities separates the extraction at the horizon, which is inviscid, from that of the viscous fluid inside the corotation radius: well below the band edge the horizon is the absorber; near the edge the viscous share is half at the operating viscosity and vanishes at the lowest. A drain-anchored horizon, as in water-tank experiments, gives the same signs. Our analysis gives insight into the mechanism of superradiance and offers a cheap, flexible platform for designing analogue-gravity experiments.

</details>

Abstract: We show how to design and build an analogue black hole as a synchronous digital circuit. Abstract: In conventional quantum mechanics, the Hamiltonian is required to be Hermitian, ensuring real energy eigenvalues. Certain non-Hermitian Hamiltonians can also possess entirely real spectra when they are invariant under the combined action of parity ($\mathcal P$) and time reversal ($\mathcal T$). Such Hamiltonians are called unbroken parity--time ($\mathcal{PT}$)-symmetric. We conjecture that, in a string-inspired scenario, a repulsive phase of gravity may be due to a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric gravitational theory, characterised by anomalous Chern-Simons terms, coupled to axion fields. The conjecture is based on the behaviour of the chiral anomalous couplings of these axions to electromagnetic potentials. Within the Exact Renormalisation Group (ERG) framework in the modified local potential approximation, we argue that singularities in the ERG beta functions for such couplings may indicate an infrared phase transition. At the energy scales of these singularities, we conjecture that the field theory transitions from the Hermitian phase to a ${\mathcal P}{\mathcal T}$-- symmetric theory, of gravity, characterised by a repulsive Netwon's ``constant''. This may induce the acceleration observed in the Universe at late eras, without the need for a dark--energy component.

Abstract: We investigate the validity of the generalized second law (GSL) of thermodynamics on the cosmological apparent horizon in $f(Q)$ symmetric teleparallel gravity, using the Wald-type entropy prefactor $Af_Q/4G$ together with a correction term derived directly from the modified Friedmann equations. Abstract: We study positivity and rigidity for an expanding quasi-local energy $E_\lambda$ associated with constantly umbilic initial data $(\Omega,g,k=\lambda g)$, introduced in earlier work [ALP26] as an analogue of the Brown-York energy adapted to an expanding flat de Sitter background. Under nonnegative scalar curvature and suitable boundary assumptions, our previous results established positivity for $\lambda$ below a geometrically determined threshold $\alpha$, while vanishing $E_\lambda$ at $\lambda=\alpha$ forced the boundary to be round. In this paper, we strengthen this endpoint rigidity statement by showing that, if $E_\lambda=0$ when $\lambda=\alpha$, then $(\Omega,g)$ is isometric to a closed Euclidean 3-ball of radius at most $1/\lambda$. The proof combines the rigidity theorem of Shi-Tam for the Brown-York energy with a strict concavity argument. We also construct an explicit counterexample to the positivity and rigidity conjecture formulated in [ALP26], showing that the energy can become negative beyond this threshold and that vanishing energy need not imply rigidity.

Abstract: We develop a quantum-computational framework for the mass observable of the Wheeler--DeWitt quantized Schwarzschild black-hole interior. Working in the physical sector spanned by correlated two-mode oscillator states, we construct finite-dimensional representations of the mass operator and map them onto qubit operators through Pauli decomposition. We analyze the resulting representations for increasing truncation sizes and establish analytically the scaling of the number of nonvanishing Pauli terms and independent measurement settings. These results provide a quantitative characterization of the quantum resources required to encode and measure the black-hole mass observable. As a representative benchmark, we implement a two-qubit variational quantum eigensolver for the smallest nontrivial truncation and find excellent agreement with the exact finite-dimensional spectrum. We further describe a quantum-circuit procedure for verifying the Wheeler--DeWitt constraint independently of the mass estimation. Our results provide a direct connection between Wheeler--DeWitt quantum gravity and quantum computation and establish a structured route toward the quantum simulation of gravitational observables.

</details>

Abstract: We study a four-dimensional charged Letelier--anti-de Sitter (AdS) black-hole metric containing a cloud of strings, a phenomenological perfect-fluid-dark-matter (PFDM) source, and a Kiselev-type quintessence term. We derive the curvature invariants, horizon temperature, entropy, electric potential, and horizon-based mass function, and analyze local thermal response and the associated extended-phase-space identities. A rectangular benchmark cycle gives $W=0.095000$ and $\eta\simeq15.39\%$ for the explicitly stated geometric-unit parameters; these values are model- and cycle-specific and are not presented as a universal efficiency enhancement. For geometric optics, we derive the photon-sphere condition, critical impact parameter, and finite-radius shadow angle. For a massless, minimally coupled scalar field, we obtain a radial-coordinate Helmholtz equation and identify frequency-dependent barriers, evanescent intervals, and candidate trapping regions; the eikonal barrier-top scale approaches the photon-sphere capture scale. Two qualifications delimit the interpretation. First, with the stated PFDM stress-tensor convention, $\rho_{\rm PFDM}=-\beta/(8\pi r^3)$, so positive $\beta$ describes a negative-density effective source. Second, the mass function follows from the horizon equation, but identifying it with a conserved asymptotic AdS mass requires a separate charge and boundary-normalization analysis. The potential-well analysis likewise does not determine complex quasinormal frequencies. We therefore present the results as a consistent, parameter-specific analysis of the adopted phenomenological geometry, while distinguishing algebraic thermodynamic identities from conclusions requiring asymptotic-charge or spectral calculations.

</details>

*Roh-Suan Tung*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从热力学第一定律出发，将引力视为热力学现象，寻求在无全局 Killing 对称性或稳态假设下，仅基于局域因果结构和能量条件导出爱因斯坦方程的严格构造；尤其解决HWZ熵在非平衡演化中缺乏单调性及对截面依赖过强的问题。

Abstract: For a twist-free null hypersurface, the Hollands-Wald-Zhang (HWZ) representative $S_{\rm loc}=(4G)^{-1}\int(1-V\theta)dA$, local on each cross-section (cut), obeys an exact off-shell Raychaudhuri identity with no term linear in the expansion, so no final equilibrium cut is needed. Given an entropy density $1/4G$, the Unruh temperature $\kappa/2\pi$ of an approximate local boost, local equilibrium cuts, and stress-tensor conservation, equating the leading equilibrium change of $S_{\rm loc}$ to matter boost heat yields $R_{kk}=8\pi GT_{kk}$ for every null direction, hence the Einstein equation with undetermined cosmological constant, an equation of state in Jacobson's conditional sense. On a fixed caustic-free generator bundle with $dA=J\,dA_{\rm ref}$, the functional $S_{\rm L}=(4G)^{-1}\int(1+\ln J-V\theta)dA_{\rm ref}$ obeys an exact balance per reference cell with curvature, shear, and expansion terms all positive, so under null convergence $S_{\rm L}$ is nondecreasing for $V\geq0$ until the first caustic. Conversely, requiring this balance for positive affine parameter on every small bundle and null direction is equivalent to the Einstein equation, given conservation and the stated normalization. Cut-locality, positivity, and first-order HWZ normalization select the density $1+\ln J-V\theta$ uniquely within a two-function class. Both functionals depend on the affine origin; we give their shift laws, identify the anchoring-invariant content, and show the origin is physical data fixed by the equilibrium cut. Near equilibrium, $S_{\rm L}$ agrees with the HWZ dynamical entropy at first order, and the temperature-weighted change of its Hessian equals the HWZ modified canonical-energy flux for stationary-vacuum perturbations. At finite amplitude, $S_{\rm L}$ is only a monotone geometric functional; reading it as entropy requires a coarse-graining postulate, stated but not derived.

</details>
