The viable parameter space is broad, and the region in which the curvaton mass and dimensionful Higgs-portal coupling are closest in scale lies within the discovery reach of proposed searches for light Higgs-mixed scalars.

</details>


### [140] [Real Intermediate States in Dark Matter Freeze-In from First Principles: Single Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10204)
*Jannis Brirup,Adrian Finke,Michael Klasen,Luca Paolo Wiggering*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论方法，首次从第一性原理出发处理s通道冻入机制中实中间态（RIS）的双重计数问题，避免了半经典玻尔兹曼方法中因有限宽度正则化导致的非幺正过计数，并系统纳入热修正；对不稳定的标量/矢量中介子采用全动量依赖的1PI自能重求和传播子，在热浴平衡假设下推导了暗物质（标量、费米子、矢量）的产生率与丰度，并数值比较了不同RIS减除方案的 phenomenological 差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s通道冻入模型中，当使用有限宽度正则化共振峰并简单叠加衰变与湮灭碰撞项时，半经典玻尔兹曼方程存在实中间态（RIS）双重计数问题；现有方案多为启发式减除，缺乏第一性原理基础，且常忽略重要热修正。

Method: 基于闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论框架，构建s通道暗物质（标量/费米子/矢量）对产生过程的完整动力学描述；对不稳定中介子采用全质量-动量依赖的1PI自能重求和传播子（超越硬热圈近似），并在热浴处于热平衡假设下导出分离演化方程。

Result: CTP方法天然规避RIS非幺正过计数；系统包含热场效应（如热质量、介质修正耦合）；给出精确到全动量依赖的中介子传播子下的冻入产率与最终暗物质丰度；数值结果表明，与常用RIS减除方案相比，CTP预测的Ωh²可偏离达O(10%)量级。

Conclusion: CTP形式主义为s通道冻入提供了自洽、幺正、热一致的第一性原理框架；RIS处理方式对预言暗物质丰度具有不可忽略的phenomenological影响，尤其在共振附近或强热效应区域；未来冻入参数分析应采用此类量子动力学方法而非启发式减除。

Abstract: We investigate the treatment of real intermediate states (RIS) in freeze-in production of $s$-channel Dark Matter (DM) with unstable mediators, as such models feature a double-counting issue when decay and annihilation collision terms are simply added within the semi-classical Boltzmann approach and a finite width is used for the regularization of the resonance. While several ad-hoc RIS-subtraction schemes are available to mitigate this problem, a first-principles treatment in this context is so far missing. We therefore start from the Closed-Time-Path (CTP) formulation of non-equilibrium Quantum Field Theory and present a CTP-based description of $s$-channel freeze-in pair production of a single scalar, fermion or vector DM species. In addition to being intrinsically free of non-unitary overcounting, the CTP ansatz also has the advantage of systematically capturing thermal corrections known to be important for DM freeze-in. Separate evolution equations assuming a single scalar or vector mediator in thermal equilibrium with the surrounding bath are presented. The exact propagators of the unstable mediators are approximated by resumming one particle irreducible (1PI) self-energy diagrams generated by bath particles and are evaluated with their full mass and momentum dependence in contrast to the commonly adopted Hard Thermal Loop limit. The CTP relic densities are compared numerically with those obtained using the most common RIS-subtraction schemes for two example $s$-channel freeze-in models, demonstrating the phenomenological relevance of a consistent RIS treatment.

</details>


### [141] [$B\to$ invisible decays as probes of light new physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10218)
*Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨B介子稀有衰变B+→K+ℰmiss中Belle II实验观测到的超出，分析其可能源于重粒子贡献的b→sνν过程或新轻粒子的壳层产生；拟合缺失质量分布倾向于2.1 GeV附近的两体末态，但0.4–0.6 GeV标量/费米子对仍可行；提出需结合B→K*ℰmiss、K*纵向极化及Bs不可见衰变等测量以提取耦合；构造一个带Higgs机制的U(1)′模型，通过矢量型顶夸克伴子实现单矢量解释，并借助轻暗费米子提供Z′不可见衰变道以规避dimuon限制，同时满足暗物质热残余丰度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Belle II在B+→K+ℰmiss中观测到的显著超出，区分新物理来源——是重粒子（如Z′）主导的b→sνν跃迁，还是新轻粒子（标量、费米子或矢量）在壳产生。

Method: 基于缺失质量谱拟合，比较单粒子（矢量Z′）、标量对（S S̄）和费米子对（χ χ̄）两类动力学假设；结合有效场论与具体模型构建（aligned Higgsed U(1)′），计算Z′-Z混合、圈诱导电弱混合、不可见宽度及暗物质热历史；利用B→K*ℰmiss分支比、K*纵向极化参数FL及Bs→μ+μ−/invisible约束。

Result: 缺失质量峰≈2.1 GeV更支持单矢量Z′解释；0.4–0.6 GeV标量/费米子对未被排除；Z′必须具有不可见衰变道（如Z′→χχ̄）以满足LHCb/CMS dimuon限制；轻暗费米子χ质量≈mZ′/2可实现共振热湮灭，匹配ΩDMh²≈0.12。

Conclusion: B+→K+ℰmiss超出最自然地由~2.1 GeV Z′玻色子解释，需辅以不可见衰变道；该Z′可通过矢量型顶夸克伴子与U(1)′规范对称性破缺实现；轻暗费米子不仅解决实验限制，还可作为热暗物质候选者；后续B→K*ℰmiss与Bs不可见衰变测量将是关键检验。

Abstract: Rare $B$ decays with missing energy probe heavy particles contributing to $b\to s\nu\bar\nu$ and new light states produced on shell. We discuss both possibilities in light of the Belle II excess in $B^+\to K^+\Emiss$. Fits to the missing-mass distribution favour a two-body contribution near $2.1\,\GeV$, while scalar and fermion pairs of masses around $0.4$-$0.6\,\GeV$ remain viable alternatives. Extracting their couplings would profit from additional measurements, including the $B\to K^*\Emiss$ rate and longitudinal $K^*$ polarization, together with invisible $B_s$ decays which constrain scalar-pair interactions. We discuss how an aligned Higgsed $U(1)'$ model can realize the single-vector explanation through a vector-like top partner. Loop-induced electroweak mixing makes visible $Z'$ decays unavoidable, requiring an additional invisible channel to evade dimuon searches. We show that a light dark fermion can provide this channel and also account for the observed dark matter abundance through resonant thermal annihilation.

</details>


### [142] [Constraints on quirks from a Geant4-based reinterpretation of the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10273)
*Tamas Almos Vami*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了CMS对重稳定带电粒子的搜索，用于Drell-Yan产生的夸克（quirks），通过Geant4模拟其在类CMS探测器中的动力学行为，并结合公开似然函数给出质量排除限；结果表明，在不同infracolor禁闭能标Λ下，对SU(2)二重态和单态quirk的质量排除范围分别为280–990 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quirks作为带有电弱荷且受远低于其质量尺度Λ的禁闭力束缚的新重费米子，其独特宏观弦状结构导致传统HSCP搜索灵敏度依赖于Λ；需系统评估CMS现有数据对quirk参数空间的约束能力。

Method: 使用Geant4对Drell-Yan产生的quirk对在参数化CMS探测器中的运动与电离响应进行蒙特卡洛模拟；精确复现CMS事件选择逻辑，并与官方发布的似然函数联合进行统计推断；对SU(2)二重态与单态、不同infracolor数目及Λ值（eV–keV）扫描排除区间。

Result: 对于SU(2)二重态quirk：当Λ ≲ 70 eV（类HSCP行为），排除质量达870/970 GeV（1/2 infracolor）；当Λ ≳ 10 keV（双电离整体运动），排除280/320 GeV；中间Λ区域因quirk在探测器内转向而无灵敏度；对FASER基准的SU(2)单态（2 infracolor），CMS在Λ < 100 eV排除至990 GeV（FASER不敏感区），Λ > 10 keV排除至300 GeV（超越FASER的175 GeV限）。

Conclusion: CMS的HSCP搜索在极低Λ（eV量级）和较高Λ（keV量级）对quirk提供强约束，填补FASER等前向实验的盲区；但中等Λ（~100 eV–1 keV）存在显著灵敏度空洞，凸显多实验协同探测的必要性。

Abstract: Quirks are new heavy fermions with electroweak charges that are also bound by a confining force whose scale $\Lambda$ is far below their mass. A produced pair therefore stays connected by a macroscopic string. We reinterpret the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles in 101 fb$^{-1}$ of 13 TeV proton-proton collisions for Drell-Yan produced quirks. The quirk dynamics is simulated with Geant4 in a parametric CMS-like detector, and the event selection is emulated and combined with the public likelihoods. The emulation reproduces the published limits on a heavy charged lepton within 11%. For an $SU(2)$-doublet quirk with one (two) infracolors, masses up to 870 (970) GeV are excluded for $\Lambda\lesssim70$ eV, where the quirks behave as independent heavy stable charged particles. For $\Lambda\gtrsim10$ keV, where the pair moves as one doubly ionizing object, the limit is 280 (320) GeV. In between, the quirks turn around inside the detector and the search has no sensitivity. For the $SU(2)$-singlet benchmark of the FASER search, with two infracolors, the limits reach 990 GeV below 100 eV, where FASER has no sensitivity, and 300 GeV above 10 keV, beyond the FASER exclusion of 175 GeV.

</details>


### [143] [A comparative analysis of semileptonic $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(η_c,B,B_s)$ decays in the light-front quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10407)
*Ho-Meoyng Choi,Bhoomika Pandya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对B(s)→D(s)和Bc→(ηc,B,Bs)半轻子衰变进行了全局分析，比较了五种基于QCD启发的光前夸克模型的势模型实现，重点考察了禁闭、径向混合与超精细相互作用对形状因子、微分衰变率、分支比及轻子味比的影响；采用统一的Bakamjian-Thomas弱流构造与固定谱学参数，通过复横动量空间中质量加权围道的直接解析延拓，在q⁺=0参考系中获得全物理类时q²区域（含零反冲点）的无参数化形状因子；最终结果以等权重模型平均与逐点极值带给出，R(D)与R(D_s)的轻子平均比值分别为0.282–0.301与0.287–0.303。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不同势模型实现下B(s)→D(s)与Bc介子半轻子衰变理论预测的系统性差异，厘清禁闭机制、径向激发混合及超精细相互作用对形状因子与可观测量（如R(D)、R(D_s)）的影响，提升标准模型预言精度并为新物理信号识别提供可靠基准。

Method: 采用五种光前夸克模型势实现，统一使用Bakamjian-Thomas框架构造弱流，固定各模型谱学参数；弱流核中使用自由夸克-反夸克运动学定义内部Lorentz前因子，外部运动学由物理介子质量决定；通过q⁺=0参考系中复横动量平面的质量加权围道对类空重叠积分作直接解析延拓，获得全q²物理类时区（含零反冲）的无参数化形状因子f₊(q²)与f₋(q²)，并验证(+,\perp)与(+,−)流分量组合对f₋(q²)的一致性。

Result: 所有模型中f₋(q²)的两种独立提取在数值精度内一致；解析延拓成功覆盖整个物理q²区间（包括零反冲点）；R(D)与R(D_s)的轻子平均比值分别为0.282–0.301与0.287–0.303，其模型依赖性被量化为逐点最小–最大带；微分衰变率与形状因子以等权重模型平均呈现。

Conclusion: 禁闭结构、径向混合与超精细处理共同主导形状因子的模型不确定性；无参数化解析延拓方法稳健可靠，显著降低q²外推误差；当前模型平均结果与实验值R(D)≈0.34及R(D_s)≈0.296存在张力，提示需进一步约束模型自由度或考虑更高阶QCD修正。

Abstract: We present a global analysis of the semileptonic transitions $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(\eta_c,B,B_s)$ by comparing five potential-model implementations of the QCD-motivated light-front quark model. We use the same fully Bakamjian--Thomas-based prescription for the weak current in all five models and keep their respective spectroscopic parameters fixed. We examine how confinement, radial mixing, and the treatment of hyperfine interactions jointly affect the form factors, differential decay rates, branching fractions, and lepton-flavor ratios. The internal Lorentz prefactors use the same free quark--antiquark kinematics as the on-shell constituent spinors in the weak-current kernels, while the physical meson masses determine the external kinematics. For each model and decay channel, the two independent extractions of $f_-(q^2)$ using the $(+,\perp)$ and $(+,-)$ current-component combinations agree within numerical accuracy. We perform a direct analytic continuation of the spacelike overlap integrals in the $q^+=0$ frame using a mass-weighted contour in complex transverse momentum space. This yields the form factors throughout the physical timelike region, including zero recoil, without parametrizing their $q^2$ dependence. Form factors and differential decay rates are presented as equal-weight model averages with pointwise minimum--maximum bands. The light-lepton-averaged ratios range from $0.282$ to $0.301$ for $R(D)$ and from $0.287$ to $0.303$ for $R(D_s)$.

</details>


### [144] [Search sensitivity for type-III seesaw heavy leptons in simulated \texorpdfstring{$pp$}{pp} collisions at \texorpdfstring{$\sqrt{s}=13.6$}{sqrt(s)=13.6} TeV at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10753)
*A.M.M. Elsayed,Yusheng Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于500 fb⁻¹的13.6 TeV pp碰撞模拟数据，利用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应，通过九个正交末态通道和多变量分析方法，对III型跷跷板机制预言的TeV尺度重轻子对产生过程开展搜寻；在味民主混合假设下，95%置信度质量排除上限约为1.6 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验III型跷跷板机制——该机制引入SU(2)_L三重态费米子（重轻子）以解释中微子小质量，并预言TeV能标可观测的重轻子对产生信号。

Method: 采用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应；按末态轻子数与大R参数喷注（large-R jets）划分为九个互斥搜索通道；应用多变量分析（MVA）提升信噪比；使用渐近卡方检验构造95% CL上限。

Result: 在味民主混合（B_e = B_μ = B_τ = 1/3）假设下，对重轻子质量m_HL的95%置信水平排除上限为≈1.6 TeV。

Conclusion: 当前LHC Run 3前期模拟灵敏度尚不足以覆盖完整理论允许参数空间，但为后续高亮度运行（HL-LHC）中重轻子直接探测提供了关键基准与分析框架。

Abstract: A search for the pair production of TeV-scale heavy leptons as predicted by the type-III seesaw mechanism is presented. The analysis is based on a simulated signal and Standard Model background data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $500~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV. The ATLAS detector response is modeled with Delphes. Events are categorized based on the number of final state leptons and large-$R$ jets into nine mutually exclusive search channels. Multivariate analysis techniques are employed to separate the signal from the Standard Model backgrounds. Under the flavor-democratic mixing scenario ($\mathcal{B}_e=\mathcal{B}_\mu=\mathcal{B}_\tau=1/3$), the expected 95\% confidence level upper exclusion limit on the heavy lepton mass is approximately $1.6$ TeV.

</details>


### [145] [Sensitivity of jet and dijet observables to medium effects in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using X-SCAPE/JETSCAPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11012)
*Lakshitha Fernando,Claude A. Pruneau,Joern Putschke,Abhijit Majumder,Victor Gonzalez,Oveis Sheibani,Akash Raj,Dana Abdallah*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架，系统研究了13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中高多重数事件的喷注淬火信号，比较了简化的Bjorken砖块介质与真实涨落初态+逐事件流体演化的两种场景，发现二喷注不变质量谱对介质效应最敏感，而其他观测量（如pT不对称性、Soft-Drop修整半径/质量）仅呈现局域化、非稳健的差异，表明当前pp体系中喷注淬火信号微弱且易受选择效应干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小系统（如高多重数pp碰撞）中确立喷注淬火现象极为困难，因预期的介质诱导修正微弱，且易被事件选择效应、多部分子相互作用及多喷注产额所掩盖。

Method: 采用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架：(1) 在简化Bjorken膨胀砖块介质模型中扫描不同长度与输运系数强度，并与真空参考对比；(2) 对比分别生成的真实初态涨落+逐事件流体演化下的真空与有介质样本（选取最高活性20%事件）；重建R=0.2与R=0.4的anti-k_T喷注；分析二喷注不变质量、领头/次领头喷注pT、折叠方位角分离、二喷注不对称性、Soft-Drop修整半径与修整质量等观测量。

Result: 在砖块模型中，二喷注不变质量谱展现出最清晰、有序的介质响应（随砖块长度与输运系数增强而单调偏离真空，且在R=0.2下更显著）；喷注pT与二喷注不对称性变化较小，未观测到显著方位角展宽；在真实事件样本中，未修整观测量的介质/真空比值近似为1，仅存在局域运动学差异及平均二喷注不对称性小幅上升；修整半径与修整质量比值亦仅显示局域差异，无持续的角窄化或质量再分布。

Conclusion: 所研究观测量对pp中小尺度介质诱导喷注修改具有可量化的有限灵敏度，其中二喷注不变质量谱是最鲁棒的探针；其余观测量在当前计算框架下未能给出稳健、普适的淬火信号，凸显小系统喷注淬火识别的理论与实验挑战。

Abstract: Establishing jet quenching in small collision systems, such as high-multiplicity proton-proton collisions at the LHC, is challenging because the expected modifications are small and can be obscured by event-selection effects, multiparton interactions, and multijet production. We examine the response of several jet and dijet observables to medium-induced changes in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using the JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE framework. First, a simplified Bjorken-expanding brick medium is compared with a vacuum reference for several brick lengths and strengths of transport coefficients. Second, separately generated realistic vacuum and medium samples with fluctuating initial conditions and event-by-event hydrodynamic evolution are compared after selecting the highest-activity 20% of events. Full anti-$k_T$ jets are reconstructed with $R=0.2$ and $R=0.4$. The observables are dijet invariant mass, leading- and subleading-jet transverse momentum, folded azimuthal separation, dijet asymmetry, Soft-Drop groomed jet radius, and groomed mass. In the brick calculation, the dijet-mass spectrum shows the clearest ordered response: departures from the vacuum reference generally increase with brick length and strength of transport coefficient and are more visible for the narrower jet radius, while the jet-$p_T$ and dijet-asymmetry changes are smaller and no clear azimuthal broadening is resolved. In the selected realistic events, the ungroomed medium-to-vacuum ratios remain close to unity but contain localized kinematic differences, together with a modest increase of the mean dijet asymmetry. The groomed-radius and groomed-mass ratios show localized differences but do not exhibit a persistent angular narrowing or mass redistribution. These findings quantify the sensitivity of the studied observables to medium-induced jet modifications in the present pp calculations.

</details>


### [146] [Quark- and hadronic-level CPT violation phenomenology of neutral-mesons at at $d=5$ of the SME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11047)
*Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了中性介子振荡中洛伦兹对称性破缺的强子层次标量有效场论描述与夸克层次d=5非最小标准模型扩展（SME）之间的对应关系，特别聚焦于一阶CPT破缺下a^{(5)}项与介子振荡中现象学CPTV参数的联系，并给出适用于K介子实验搜寻的一般结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立强子层次有效理论与夸克层次SME之间在Lorentz/CPT破坏框架下的严格对应，以统一不同能标下的现象学解释并提升实验可检验性。

Method: 基于C、P、T对称性及QCD手征对称性自发破缺在两种形式体系中均应成立的物理前提，在一阶CPT破坏近似下，通过算符匹配与对称性约束推导强子化标量有效拉氏量与夸克层次d=5 SME系数a^{(5)}μνρ之间的映射关系。

Result: 确立了强子层次CPTV现象学参数与夸克层次a^{(5)}系数的定量对应；导出适用于K⁰–K̄⁰系统CPTV效应分析的一般解析表达式。

Conclusion: 强子层次标量EFT与d=5非最小SME在CPT破坏框架下具有一致性；该对应为利用中性介子振荡实验（尤其是K介子）约束高维SME系数提供了坚实的理论桥梁。

Abstract: This article summarizes an investigation of the connection between the hadron-level scalar effective field theory treatment of Lorentz symmetry violation in neutral-meson oscillations and the corresponding quark level $d=5$ nonminimal SME approach. The correspondence between the quark-level $a^{(5)}$ term is identified to first order in CPT violation (CPTV) based on the expectation that C, P, T symmetries and the spontaneously broken chiral symmetry of QCD should hold for both formalism. These can be related to the phenomenological parameter describing possible CPTV in the meson oscillations. A general result facilitating searches for kaons is given.

</details>


### [147] [Study of the weak radiative decay $B_c^+\to B_u^{\ast+}γ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11095)
*Jia Zhao,Tianhong Wang,Xi-Yu Ni,Zhi-Hui Wang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型框架下，利用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方法研究稀有辐射衰变 $B_c 	o B_u^*\gamma$，系统计入短距penguin、弱湮灭与长距矢量介子主导（VMD）贡献；发现LD部分对$\rho$宽度敏感，SD贡献与LD相当但远大于湮灭项，表明该过程是探测粲夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）的重要探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粲底介子 $B_c$ 到激发态轻介子 $B_u^*$ 的稀有辐射衰变，以检验标准模型中粲夸克的味改变中性流（FCNC）机制，并评估不同QCD贡献（短距、长距、湮灭）的相对重要性。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter（BS）波函数描述强子结构，结合有效哈密顿量计算短距 $c \to u\gamma$ penguin 图、弱湮灭图及长距矢量介子主导（VMD）机制；对VMD中的 $\rho$ 介子宽度分别取零与实验值以考察其影响。

Result: 长距贡献在 $\Gamma_\rho = 0$ 和 $\Gamma_\rho = \Gamma_\rho^{\rm exp}$ 下分别为 $5.0\times10^{-9}$ 和 $7.3\times10^{-8}$；短距贡献与长距同量级，而弱湮灭小一个数量级；总分支比由SD与LD共同主导。

Conclusion: $B_c \to B_u^*\gamma$ 是研究粲夸克FCNC过程的高灵敏探针，其衰变率对强子化机制（尤其是VMD中 $\rho$ 宽度）高度敏感，BS方法可自洽处理非微扰强子结构效应。

Abstract: We investigate the rare radiative decay $B_c \to B_u^\ast\gamma$ within the Standard Model by using the instantaneous Bethe-Salpeter (BS) approach. The short distance (SD) $c \to u\gamma$ penguin, weak annihilation, and long distance (LD) vector meson dominance (VMD) contributions are all included. In the calculation of LD contribution, we use two scenarios, namely the decay width of $\rho$ being set to zero or kept as the experimental value, which gives $\mathrm{Br}_{\rm LD}=5.0\times10^{-9}$ and $7.3\times10^{-8}$, respectively, indicating an important role of $\Gamma_\rho$ in the calculation. The SD contribution is comparable in magnitude to the LD contributions, but one order larger than the annihilation contribution. This shows the $B_c \to B_u^\ast\gamma$ channel can provide an important way to study the flavor changing neutral current processes of charm quark.

</details>


### [148] [Holographic dissociation of tetraquark and hybrid meson in the spinning plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11202)
*Zhenqiang Ren,Xiang Gao,Zhou-Run Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋Myers-Perry黑洞的全息对偶，研究四夸克态Zc和杂化介子π₁在自旋夸克胶子等离子体中的解离行为；采用非二次 dilaton 剖面拟合其质量谱，计算纵向与横向谱函数，发现温度与自旋（boost参数）均导致峰高降低、峰宽展宽（横向更显著），且2S态先于1S态熔解；两种奇特强子表现出明显不同的解离模式，提供统一全息框架下比较其热稳定性的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质在高温高密环境下奇特强子（如四夸克态Zc与杂化介子π₁）的热稳定性与解离机制，亟需一个能同时描述多种非传统强子态的统一全息框架。

Method: 基于AdS/QCD的全息方法，采用平面极限下的自旋Myers-Perry黑洞背景模拟自旋夸克胶子等离子体；引入非二次dilaton场剖面，通过拟合Zc与π₁的质量谱确定其参数；在纵向与横向通道中计算相应的谱函数以提取解离行为。

Result: 温度与boost参数均抑制谱函数峰值、展宽峰宽（横向效应更强）；2S激发态比对应1S基态更早解离；Zc（四夸克）与π₁（杂化介子）在同一全息框架下展现出定性不同的解离动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个统一全息框架，可不依赖于微扰或动力学绑定能计算，直接比较不同类奇特强子（四夸克vs.杂化介子）在强耦合QGP中的热致解离行为，凸显全息方法在非微扰强子物理中的结构性优势。

Abstract: We explore the dissociation of the tetraquark \(Z_c\) and the hybrid meson \(\pi_1\) in a spinning quark-gluon plasma described by the planar limit of a spinning Myers-Perry black hole. Both states are treated within one holographic framework, using a non-quadratic dilaton profile whose parameters are fixed by the \(Z_c\) and \(\pi_1\) mass spectra. Spectral functions are computed in the longitudinal and transverse channels. The boost parameter and temperature both lower the peak height and broaden the width, and the effect is stronger in the transverse channel. In both channels, the heavier 2S states melt before the 1S states. Furthermore, the two exotic channels display distinct dissociation behaviors within the same effective framework. This provides a unified holographic approach to compare how different exotic states behavior, without requiring a dynamical calculation of their binding energies.

</details>


### [149] [Precision Fragmentation Functions from Modern COMPASS SIDIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11237)
*Maryam Soleymaninia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种带电π介子、K介子和未鉴别带电强子的共线碎裂函数（FFs）全局拟合结果（HAPS-PiFF1.0、HAPS-KaFF1.0、HAPS-hFF1.0），融合了e⁺e⁻单包含湮灭与电荷分离的半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）数据（含2025年COMPASS质子靶与2026年修订的等标靶结果），采用神经网络参数化、蒙特卡洛复本及NLO/NNLO微扰计算框架；质子与等标靶数据提升了味分辨能力，而胶子碎裂仍受限；文中还讨论了微扰精度影响、主要局限性及未来测量前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升强子碎裂函数（FFs）的精度与味分辨能力，尤其在胶子碎裂通道约束不足的背景下，通过融合最新SIDIS与e⁺e⁻数据，并利用不同靶核（质子/等标）增强夸克味敏感性。

Method: 采用灵活的神经网络参数化形式、蒙特卡洛复本法进行误差传播，并在NLO与NNLO QCD微扰理论框架下执行全局QCD拟合；联合分析单包含e⁺e⁻湮灭与电荷分离的SIDIS数据（含COMPASS 2025质子靶与2026修订等标靶数据）。

Result: 给出了π⁺/π⁻、K⁺/K⁻及未鉴别带电强子的首套HAPS系列共线碎裂函数（PiFF1.0/KaFF1.0/hFF1.0），显著改善了u/d/s夸克碎裂的约束，质子与等标靶SIDIS数据协同增强了味分离能力，但胶子→强子碎裂仍存在较大不确定性。

Conclusion: 当前全局拟合在夸克碎裂方面取得显著进展，但胶子碎裂函数仍为主要系统误差源；NNLO修正带来可观的理论稳定性提升；未来需更高统计量的电荷分离SIDIS数据（尤其中子靶/重核靶）及e⁺e⁻角分布高阶测量以进一步约束胶子通道。

Abstract: We present a comparative account of three global determinations of collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for charged pions, charged kaons, and unidentified charged hadrons. The HAPS-PiFF1.0, HAPS-KaFF1.0, and HAPS-hFF1.0 analyses combine single-inclusive $e^+e^-$ annihilation with charge-separated semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS), including the 2025 COMPASS proton-target measurements and the revised 2026 isoscalar-target results. Flexible neural-network parametrizations, Monte Carlo replicas, and NLO/NNLO perturbative setups are employed. The proton and isoscalar samples sharpen the flavor sensitivity of SIDIS, while gluon fragmentation remains comparatively weakly constrained. We discuss the effects of perturbative accuracy, the principal limitations, and prospects for future measurements.

</details>


### [150] [An $SU(5)$ GUT with non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ symmetry: fermion masses, mixing and leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11241)
*Xianshuo Zhang,Yakefu Reyimuaji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于非全纯模对称性 $S'_4$ 和整体Peccei-Quinn $U(1)_{PQ}$ 对称性的非超对称 $SU(5)$ 大统一模型，利用多调和Maass形式构造Yukawa耦合，成功拟合全部费米子质量与混合角（含中微子振荡参数NO/IO），并预言绝对中微子质量、CP相位及无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量；通过密度矩阵方程研究热轻子生成，发现重子不对称度仅由右手中微子质量标度参数 $b$ 决定，并可精确复现观测值 $\eta_B \simeq 6.12 \times 10^{-10}$，建立了味物理与早期宇宙学的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中费米子质量与混合模式的起源问题，同时协调大统一理论、中微子物理与重子不对称性生成机制，避免超对称引入的额外自由度。

Method: 采用非全纯 $S'_4$ 模对称性与 $U(1)_{PQ}$ 全局对称性联合约束Yukawa结构；Yukawa耦合由polyharmonic Maass forms显式构造；中微子质量通过类型-I seesaw机制实现；热轻子生成通过求解包含味效应的密度矩阵方程进行数值分析。

Result: 模型在NO和IO下均以 $|\mathcal{P}| < 0.8$ 精确拟合所有已知费米子观测量；给出可检验的中微子绝对质量尺度、$\delta_{CP}$、Majorana相及 $m_{\beta\beta}$ 预言；热轻子生成中 $\eta_B$ 由单一参数 $b$ 完全决定，且可精确匹配观测值；揭示 $b$ 与低能味可观测量间的严格相关性。

Conclusion: 非超对称 $SU(5)$ GUT 在非全纯模对称性框架下可自洽统一描述味物理与重子生成，$S'_4$ 模结构与 $U(1)_{PQ}$ 约束共同确保预测能力与自然性，为连接TeV能标味实验与宇宙学观测提供了新范式。

Abstract: A non-supersymmetric $SU(5)$ grand unified model is constructed based on the non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ flavor symmetry, supplemented by a global Peccei--Quinn $U(1)_{PQ}$ symmetry that constrains the Yukawa couplings. Constructed from polyharmonic Maaß forms, these couplings allow the model to reproduce the observed charged-fermion masses and mixing as well as the neutrino oscillation parameters for both normal ordering (NO) and inverted ordering (IO), with all Pull values satisfying $|\mathcal{P}| < 0.8$. The model further yields specific predictions for yet-unmeasured parameters, including the absolute neutrino masses, the Dirac CP phase, the Majorana phases, and the effective Majorana mass $m_{\beta\beta}$. We then study thermal leptogenesis by solving the density matrix equations. For a given benchmark point, the final baryon asymmetry depends solely on the single free parameter $b$ that sets the right-handed neutrino mass scale, and an appropriate choice of this parameter reproduces the observed value $\eta_B \simeq 6.12 \times 10^{-10}$. Finally, the correlation between this scale parameter and specific low-energy observables provides a potential bridge between the fermion flavor structure and early-Universe cosmology.

</details>


### [151] [Direct Light-Cone Reconstruction of the $B$-Meson Shape Function in HQET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11246)
*Ao-Sheng Xiong,Shu-Man Hu,Ji Xu,Fu-Sheng Yu,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在重夸克有效理论（HQET）框架下定义的B介子准形状函数，并计算了其在一阶圈图水平上到光锥形状函数的匹配，避免了通过全QCD中间步骤；结果给出了准形状函数在MS-bar和RI/MOM方案下的单圈重整化核与匹配系数，为格点HQET直接提取B介子形状函数提供了必要的微扰输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQET在重夸克质量可比拟或超过格点截断动量尺度时，为格点计算提供了自然框架；为实现格点上对B介子形状函数的直接非微扰提取，需构造一个可在格点上定义且能与光锥形状函数系统匹配的准分布。

Method: 在HQET中直接定义B介子准形状函数（基于空间分离算符的矩阵元），采用维度正规化与最小减除（MS-bar）或RI/MOM方案进行重整化；在一阶圈图水平上完成准形状函数到光锥形状函数的匹配，保留大v^z展开的领头阶项，并证明匹配系数与红外正规化无关。

Result: 得到了准形状函数在MS-bar与RI/MOM方案下的单圈重整化核；获得了领头阶大v^z展开下的匹配系数，该系数不依赖于红外正规化方式；所有匹配系数均以解析形式给出，适用于后续格点HQET模拟的数据分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的HQET准形状函数及其单圈匹配关系，构成了格点上直接、可靠地提取B介子非微扰形状函数的完整微扰基础，显著简化了传统需经全QCD中间匹配的路径。

Abstract: The $B$-meson shape function encodes the nonperturbative residual-momentum dynamics of the heavy quark within heavy quark effective theory (HQET). From a Wilsonian perspective, HQET provides a natural framework for lattice calculations when the heavy quark mass is comparable to or exceeds the inverse lattice spacing. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a $B$-meson quasi-shape function directly in HQET and calculate its one-loop matching to the light-cone shape function, avoiding an intermediate matching step involving a full-QCD light-cone distribution. We determine the one-loop renormalization kernels and matching coefficients in both the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ and regularization-independent momentum-subtraction (RI/MOM) schemes for the quasi-shape function, while keeping the light-cone shape function in the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme. The matching coefficients are obtained at leading power in the large-$v^z$ expansion and are independent of the infrared regulator. Our results provide the perturbative ingredients for a direct lattice HQET determination of the $B$-meson shape function.

</details>


### [152] [Probing the Supersymmetric Grand Unified Theories with Anomaly Mediation at the Future Proton-Proton Colliders and Hyper-Kamiokande Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11254)
*Waqas Ahmed,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在大沙漠假设下，研究了基于最小反常介质超对称破缺（AMSB）机制的超对称大统一理论（SUSY GUTs），重点探讨其在100 TeV强子对撞机（FCC-hh/SppC）与Hyper-Kamiokande质子衰变实验中的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续前期对SUSY GUTs在极高能对撞机与质子衰变实验中可检验性的系统研究，旨在结合大沙漠假设与最小AMSB机制，建立可被未来实验证伪的理论框架。

Method: 采用大统一尺度MGUT作为自由参数，在最小AMSB框架下计算超粒子质量谱（特别是胶微子、第一二代标量夸克、带电轻子、赝标量Higgs及电弱微子），并结合RGE演化、质子衰变（维度六算符）寿命公式与探测阈值，确定MGUT临界值0.84×10¹⁶ GeV及其对应的实验可探性边界。

Result: 发现当MGUT ≥ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，胶微子质量上限为8 TeV、第一二代标量夸克为6 TeV，可在FCC-hh/SppC中探测；当MGUT ≤ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，维度六质子衰变寿命≤ ~5×10³⁴年，可被Hyper-Kamiokande覆盖；文中给出两类典型基准点以表征该临界行为。

Conclusion: 最小AMSB SUSY GUT模型在大沙漠假设下具有清晰的可检验性：其超粒子谱与质子衰变寿命在MGUT = 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV处发生关键分界，使得100 TeV对撞机与Hyper-Kamiokande构成互补探测窗口，为未来高能物理实验提供明确理论指引。

Abstract: With the grand desert hypothesis, we investigate the Supersymmetric (SUSY) Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) with the minimal anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB) as a continuation of our program of studying SUSY GUTs at the future 100 TeV proton-proton Collider such as $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC as well as proton-decay experiment such as Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. We show that for the GUT scale $M_{GUT} \ge 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the upper mass bounds on the gluino and first two generation squarks are 8 TeV and 6 TeV, respectively. Therefore, the gluino and first two generation squarks can be probed at the $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC. In addition, we discuss the mass spectra for the colored sparticles, sleptons, pseudo-scalar Higgs and electroweakinos. We also shed light on the possible search strategies of gluino and winos at the future 100 TeV colliders. Moreover, for $\rm M_{\rm GUT} \le 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the proton lifetime for dimension-six proton decay via heavy gauge boson exchange is less than or around $5 \times 10^{34}$ years, and thus we can probe it at the Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. To be concrete, we present a couple of benchmark points representing the above two mentioned GUT conditions on $\rm M_{\rm GUT}$.

</details>


### [153] [Rapid, High-Precision Numerical Evaluation of Multiloop Feynman Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11292)
*Richard Easther*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Sinc函数逼近法将多圈费曼图积分转化为快速收敛的多维求和，并在电子反常磁矩(g-2)计算中与蒙特卡洛方法对比，显示其在高精度（如六位有效数字）下显著优于后者；该方法已成功应用于五圈图，并具备推广潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升多圈费曼图数值计算的精度与效率，尤其针对电子g-2这类高精度理论预言与实验比对的关键可观测量，克服传统蒙特卡洛方法在高精度下计算成本指数增长的瓶颈。

Method: 采用Sinc函数插值与数值积分变换技术，将原始发散或慢收敛的Feynman参数积分映射为指数收敛的多重离散求和；结合解析处理（如UV/IR分离、张量约化）与自适应网格优化。

Result: Sinc方法在~1%精度下与蒙特卡洛相当；在六位有效数字精度下，三圈图计算耗时为分钟量级，而蒙特卡洛预估需数年；首次实现单个五圈g-2积分的数值评估；提出外推加速策略并验证其有效性。

Conclusion: Sinc函数数值方法是一种高效、高精度、可扩展的多圈费曼积分计算新范式，适用于电子g-2等关键精密QED计算，并有望推广至更一般拓扑结构的费曼图。

Abstract: We numerically evaluate multiloop Feynman diagrams by using Sinc function approximations to transform the integrals into rapidly convergent, multidimensional sums. We compare the efficiency of this approach to Monte Carlo evaluations of electron g-2 terms. We show that the two strategies reach parity at precisions of around 1% for the full sets of three and four loop diagrams. Beyond this level Sinc function methods are dramatically faster - computing 3-loop integrals to six digit precision takes minutes whereas Monte Carlo timings map to years. We show that the method can evaluate individual 5-loop g-2 integrals and discuss extrapolation techniques that would further improve performance. We note that the method appears to be applicable to more general diagrams.

</details>


### [154] [Probing Exotic Electron-Electron Interactions via Adiabatic Spin Inversion in MRFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11367)
*Chunlong Li,Shien Yang,Yiwei Huang,Yu Zhang,Fei Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于磁共振力显微镜（MRFM）与OSCAR技术的新型探测方案，用于灵敏搜寻超越标准模型的轻玻色子介导的短程自旋依赖相互作用，尤其对V₂、V₃和V₈类势具有理论可观测信号，并预计可在亚微米尺度改进对V₂的现有实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型之外的轻玻色子（如轴子、暗光子等）所介导的电子-电子间短程自旋依赖相互作用，此类相互作用在传统磁测量中易被背景淹没，亟需高空间分辨、低噪声、无磁屏蔽的新型探测范式。

Method: 采用振荡悬臂驱动的绝热自旋反转（OSCAR）技术，在MRFM中利用铁磁针尖作为极化自旋源与磁场梯度源；通过线性化自旋-力学耦合动力学，解析推导V₂、V₃势导致的共振频率偏移及V₈势诱导的二倍频力信号；引入中断式OSCAR差分检测与距离扫描策略以抑制常规磁背景。

Result: 理论推导出三类自旋势（V₂、V₃、V₈）对应的可测力学响应：V₂/V₃引起共振频率漂移，V₈产生二倍频力；差分检测与距离依赖测量可有效区分新物理信号与磁偶极背景；基于典型单自旋/系综MRFM参数的灵敏度估计表明，该方案在0.1–1 μm范围对V₂耦合强度的约束可达gₑ²/4π ≲ 10⁻³⁰，优于当前部分短程实验。

Conclusion: 绝热自旋翻转MRFM是一种具备空间局域性、本征背景抑制能力与高力学灵敏度的新探测平台，为检验电子层次的超轻新物理提供了互补且具竞争力的实验路径。

Abstract: Light bosons beyond the Standard Model can mediate exotic spin-dependent interactions between electrons, motivating sensitive searches at short distances. We propose a magnetic resonance force microscopy (MRFM) scheme that exploits oscillating-cantilever-driven adiabatic reversals (OSCAR) of electron spins to convert these interactions into measurable mechanical signals. The ferromagnetic tip serves as both a dense polarized-spin source and a source of magnetic-field gradients, enabling force detection without magnetic shielding between the source and sensor. Within a linearized treatment of the coupled spin-mechanical dynamics, we derive resonance-frequency shifts associated with the $V_2$ and $V_3$ interactions and a second-harmonic force signal arising from the velocity-dependent $V_8$ interaction. We further investigate interrupted-OSCAR differential detection and distance-dependent measurements to discriminate exotic signals from conventional magnetic backgrounds. Using representative parameters from ensemble and single-spin MRFM experiments, we estimate the projected sensitivity under specified noise and statistical assumptions. The results suggest that the proposed scheme could provide complementary sensitivity to short-range spin-dependent interactions, with the potential to improve existing constraints on $V_2$ over selected submicrometer interaction ranges. Our study establishes adiabatic spin-inversion MRFM as a promising approach to probing exotic electron-electron forces.

</details>


### [155] [How to isolate charm production mechanisms using charm-anticharm rapidity and azimuthal angle differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11422)
*Marcus O'Riordan,Peter Christiansen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用PYTHIA模拟研究了强子对撞中粲夸克对（D⁰D̄⁰）的关联特性，发现硬散射、末态劈裂和初态辐射产生的粲夸克在快度差Δy和方位角差Δφ上具有显著可分性；通过快度分离选择可调控末态劈裂成分，为LHC及重离子碰撞中区分粲夸克产生机制及QGP中重夸克与胶子的不同介质效应提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲夸克产生过程复杂（含硬散射、初/末态辐射等），难以从第一性原理计算，需实验可观测量以分辨不同产生机制及其在QGP中的演化差异。

Method: 基于PYTHIA事件生成器模拟，分析D⁰–D̄⁰对在快度差Δy与方位角差Δφ相空间中的分布特征，识别各产生机制（hard-process, final-state splitting, initial-state shower）的相空间印记。

Result: 硬散射主导中快度相近（|Δy|小）但方位角接近背靠背（|Δφ|≈π）；末态劈裂贡献集中在近侧（|Δφ|≈0）且中等|Δy|；初态辐射则倾向大|Δy|；Δy选切可显著压低或增强末态劈裂成分；LHCb Run 1数据支持该方法可行性。

Conclusion: D⁰D̄⁰关联的微分测量（尤其Δy谱）是区分粲夸克产生机制的有效实验工具，可在pp和AA碰撞中分别约束胶子劈裂动力学与QGP中c-夸克 vs. 胶子输运性质。

Abstract: Charm production in hadronic collisions proceeds via many different processes, which makes it hard to calculate from first principles. Using PYTHIA simulations, we study correlations between charm and anticharm hadrons and show that hard-process charm, final-state splitting charm, and initial-state-shower charm populate markedly different regions of rapidity and azimuthal-angle difference. In particular, selections on the pair rapidity separation, $\Delta y$, can strongly enrich or suppress the near-side final-state splitting contribution and thereby provide experimental sensitivity to the different production processes. A comparison with existing LHCb $D^0\bar{D}^0$ correlation measurements from Run 1 motivates more differential studies using the substantially larger LHC data sets now available. Extending such measurements to heavy-ion collisions could further distinguish medium modifications of charm produced directly in the hard scattering from charm originating from a parent gluon, providing a new handle on the different interactions of heavy quarks and gluons with the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [156] [After the Higgs: Looking for the Universe Behind the Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11458)
*Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了希格斯玻色子从发现目标转变为探索自然真空、检验量子场论及寻找新物理原理的关键工具的过程，并讨论了在缺乏理论保证新物理可及性的情况下，基础科学研究应如何规划未来探索路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在希格斯玻色子发现十四年后，其角色已从被搜寻对象转变为探测自然真空、验证量子场论和探索新物理原理的精密探针；同时，当前缺乏理论依据保证新物理在实验可达范围内，且基础科学长期资助日益困难。

Method: 通过回顾希格斯物理从理论驱动发现到精度测量范式的演进，分析其作为标量场的独特量子性质（如非平凡真空期望值、自相互作用结构、与宇宙学常数及暴胀场的潜在联系），并结合科研战略层面的反思，提出应对不确定性的方法论：以无预设偏好的高精度测量为核心，维持实验多样性，保护理论冒险精神，并独立论证基础探索的价值。

Result: 阐明希格斯场作为唯一已知的基本标量场，其质量参数、自耦合强度、CP性质及与引力/宇宙学的耦合构成通往超越标准模型物理（如电弱相变、暗物质起源、早期宇宙动力学）的关键通道；同时指出，面对‘无定理保障发现’的新常态，粒子物理需转向以精度、多样性与思想开放性为支柱的探索范式。

Conclusion: 希格斯玻色子不仅是标准模型的收官之钥，更是通向更深层自然规律的门户；未来研究不应依赖于特定新物理模型的预言，而应依托其内在独特性开展多维度精密检验，并在科学政策与公众沟通中重申基础科学探索本身即为目的的价值观。

Abstract: Fourteen years after its discovery, the Higgs boson has changed roles. It was once the object of the search, but today it is an instrument with which we interrogate Nature's vacuum, test quantum field theory and look for new principles. This shift comes at a time when, unlike before the Higgs discovery, we have no theoretical argument guaranteeing new physics within experimental reach. At the same time, securing long-term support for fundamental science is becoming increasingly challenging. I revisit the path from a theoretically motivated discovery to a precision programme, explain why the Higgs is such an unusual quantum field, and argue that this same peculiarity makes it a gateway to cosmology and other sectors. I then turn to a less technical question: how should a field plan for exploration when no theorem guarantees the next discovery? My answer is to pursue precision without pretending to know in advance which measurement will matter, sustain a diverse experimental portfolio, protect theoretical risk-taking, and make the public case for fundamental exploration on its own terms.

</details>


### [157] [Short-range dynamics of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ in a coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11472)
*Fang-Zheng Peng,Li-Sheng Geng,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究X(3872)与T_{cc}(3875)^+的短程动力学，采用耦合道S波DD*与D\bar{D}*描述，结合矢量介子交换饱和、显式π交换（减去局域项）及D*自能（含D\bar{D}π/ DDπ三体效应）。通过T_{cc}极点固定接触相互作用，扫描X(3872)极点对磁偶极项的依赖；发现完整保留该局域矢量项可得最浅束缚态（仍低于实验质量5–12 MeV）；π交换显著影响X(3872)同位旋破缺；Feshbach投影揭示纯分子图像存在张力，表明T_{cc}需引入紧致cc\bar{u}\bar{d}组分混合；BO-EFT分析显示X(3872)与T_{cc}中短距成分权重分别为3.0–9.7%与1.3–14.4%，支持在饱和框架中保留局域矢量交换，并倡导分子与紧致自由度联合描述T_{cc}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和X(3872)与T_{cc}(3875)^+在纯分子图像下的参数张力，探究其是否共用同一短程相互作用机制，并厘清紧致四夸克态与分子态的相对贡献。

Method: 耦合道Lippmann-Schwinger方程，含D*自能（三体D\bar{D}π/ DDπ）、矢量介子交换饱和约束下的接触项、显式π交换（减局域项）；Feshbach投影分析通道混合；Born-Oppenheimer有效场论提取空间结构与组分权重。

Result: 1) X(3872)极点对局域矢量磁偶极项敏感，完整保留时得最浅束缚态（仍低于实验值5–12 MeV）；2) π交换对X(3872)同位旋破缺影响强于对束缚能的影响；3) Feshbach分析表明T_{cc}不能由纯DD*分子解释，需混入紧致cc\bar{u}\bar{d}态；4) BO-EFT给出X(3872)与T_{cc}中短距成分权重分别为3.0–9.7%与1.3–14.4%。

Conclusion: X(3872)与T_{cc}(3875)^+共享相似短程动力学框架，但T_{cc}本质为DD*分子与紧致四夸克态的量子混合态；局域矢量交换项应在饱和框架中保留；二者需统一处理分子与紧致自由度，不可孤立采用纯分子图像。

Abstract: We study the short-range dynamics in the coupled-channel description of the $S$-wave $D \bar{D}^*$ and $DD^*$ systems for $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$, respectively. Contact interactions are related through scalar- and vector-meson exchange saturation, while pion exchange is included explicitly with its local term subtracted. The coupled Lippmann-Schwinger equations incorporate the self-energy of the unstable $D^*$ mesons, thereby accounting for the three-body $D \bar{D} \pi$ ($D D\pi$) dynamics. Fixing the contact interaction from the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ pole, we trace the predicted $X(3872)$ pole as the local magnetic dipole term from vector-meson exchange is varied. Retaining this term in full produces the shallowest poles, which remain approximately $5$--$12~\mathrm{MeV}$ below the observed $X(3872)$ mass. It is found that the pion exchange has a moderate effect on the binding energy and a stronger effect on the isospin violation for the $X(3872)$ state. The residual tension between the pure-molecular bound descriptions of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is analyzed with the Feshbach projection. This analysis indicates a $DD^*$ interaction weaker than that implied by the purely molecular fits, and shows that the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ state instead arises from the mixing of the $DD^{*}$ channel with a compact $cc\bar{u}\bar{d}$ state. We further examine the spatial structure using Born--Oppenheimer effective field theory. For separation radii of $0.5$--$1.5~\mathrm{fm}$, the combined charmonium and short-distance tetraquark weight in $X(3872)$ is $3.0$--$9.7\%$, while the short-distance weight in $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is $1.3$--$14.4\%$. These results favor retaining the local vector-exchange contribution within the saturation framework and motivate a joint treatment of molecular and compact dynamics for the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$.

</details>


### [158] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to radiative decays of fully heavy tetraquarks into quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11511)
*Yu-Dong Zhang,Zai-Fu Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD因子化框架下，计算了紧致四夸克态 $T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q$（$Q=c,b$）到S波偶素加光子衰变的NLO QCD修正，给出了解析形式的短距系数，发现NLO修正显著增强衰变宽度（因子~1.8–2.9），并预估LHC上可观的产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究紧致四夸克态 $T_{4Q}$ 的电磁衰变是检验其内部结构（如双夸克-双反夸克色单态/八重态组态）、验证NRQCD因子化适用性、并为实验搜寻（如LHC的 $J/\psi+\gamma$ 不变质量谱）提供理论基准的关键步骤。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，在重夸克速度展开的领头阶（$v^0$）下，对 $T_{4Q}^{J^{PC}} \to V_Q/\eta_Q + \gamma$ 过程计算NLO QCD修正（$\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^5)$）；显式处理两种颜色构型（单态/八重态）干涉；通过质量与强耦合常数重整化精确抵消紫外发散。

Result: 解析得到所有通道的短距系数；NLO修正使 $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}\to V_Q+\gamma$ 宽度增强约2.94（2.33），$T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}\to\eta_Q+\gamma$ 增强约2.22（1.83）；重整化尺度依赖减弱但仍显著；预言LHC 13 TeV下 $\mathcal{L}=100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ 时 $T_{4c}\to J/\psi+\gamma$ 产额达 $10^6$–$10^7$，$T_{4c}\to\eta_c+\gamma$ 约 $10^4$。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正对 $T_{4Q}$ 电磁衰变至关重要，显著提升理论预测精度与可靠性；$J/\psi+\gamma$ 末态是LHC上探测紧致 $T_{4c}$ 态的高灵敏探针，可与双粲偶素搜寻互补。

Abstract: Within nonrelativistic QCD factorization, we calculate the next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections to the decays of compact $T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ states ($Q=c,b$) into an $S$-wave quarkonium and a photon. Conservation of charge conjugation allows $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$ and $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}$ to decay into $V_Q+\gamma$, with $V_c=J/\psi$ and $V_b=\Upsilon$, while $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}$ can decay into $\eta_Q+\gamma$. At leading order in the heavy quark velocity expansion, we derive the short-distance coefficients for these decay widths in analytic form through $\mathcal{O}(\alpha\alpha_s^5)$, including the interference contribution between the two color configurations of $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$. All ultraviolet divergences at NLO are exactly canceled by renormalization. The NLO QCD corrections enhance the decay widths for $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}\to V_Q+\gamma$ and $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}\to\eta_Q+\gamma$ by factors of approximately $2.94$ ($2.33$) and $2.22$ ($1.83$), respectively. The dependence on the renormalization scale $\mu_R$ is reduced at NLO in most channels, although it remains sizable. Based on the NLO branching fractions, we estimate yields of order $10^6$--$10^7$ for $T_{4c}\to J/\psi+\gamma$ and $10^4$ for $T_{4c}\to\eta_c+\gamma$ in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13~\mathrm{TeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The $J/\psi+\gamma$ channels can be investigated through $J/\psi\to\ell^+\ell^-$ in the $\ell^+\ell^-\gamma$ invariant-mass spectrum, complementing di-charmonium searches.

</details>


### [159] [Spin entanglement in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions: high-energy TMD effects and fragmentation probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11568)
*Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在k_T-因子化与冲击波框架下，研究质子-质子/质子-核碰撞中产生的夸克-反夸克对的自旋-自旋关联；推导了背对背极限下包含与衍射通道的完整自旋密度矩阵；发现包含道中线偏振胶子诱导cos(2φ)调制的量子纠缠度量（如concurrence、Rényi熵、量子失谐），其强度受小-x胶子TMDs调控，并在BFKL极限下最强、与横向动量失衡q无关，但在饱和效应下显著压低；衍射道中夸克对始终纠缠，其性质源于Pomeron交换的幺正性与排它性，对应秩≤2、边缘态最大混合的自旋密度矩阵，此时纠缠与贝尔非定域性等价；提出通过Collins及双强子碎裂函数在非极化强子碰撞中探测该自旋关联，并指出对无质量夸克，特定双Collins不对称性可作为强纠缠见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能强子碰撞中夸克-反夸克对的量子纠缠特性，揭示小-x QCD动力学（特别是胶子极化与饱和）对自旋关联的影响，并探索在非极化实验中观测量子纠缠的新途径。

Method: 采用k_T-因子化框架结合冲击波（shockwave）形式论，构建背对背极限下inclusive与diffractive通道的完整自旋密度矩阵；系统分析线偏振胶子贡献、六个小-x胶子TMDs的干涉效应；利用concurrence、稳定器Rényi熵、量子失谐、贝尔不等式检验等量子信息度量刻画纠缠结构；结合光前螺旋度振幅与Pomeron交换的幺正/排它性进行解析推导。

Result: 1) Inclusive道：出现由线偏振胶子驱动的cos(2φ)调制的量子纠缠信号，其幅度由六个小-x胶子TMDs共同决定；BFKL极限下该调制达最大且与q无关，而CGC饱和效应强烈压制该信号；2) Diffractive道：夸克对始终处于纠缠态，其自旋密度矩阵秩≤2、单粒子约化态最大混合，导致纠缠与贝尔非定域性严格等价，且在整个相空间普适成立；3) 提出双Collins不对称性作为无质量夸克情形下的鲁棒纠缠见证。

Conclusion: 夸克-反夸克对的自旋-自旋关联是探测量子纠缠与小-x QCD非线性动力学（如胶子饱和）的全新探针；inclusive与diffractive通道展现出截然不同的纠缠结构，分别反映胶子横向动量分布与Pomeron交换的幺正本质；实验上可通过现有强子碎裂观测量实现无须极化束流的纠缠探测。

Abstract: Spin-spin correlations of a quark-antiquark pair produced in proton-proton or proton-Ion collisions are investigated in $k_T$-factorization within the shockwave formalism. We construct the full spin-density matrix of both inclusive and diffractive channels in the limit in which the two quarks are back-to-back. In the inclusive channel, due to linearly polarized gluons, we show that a $\cos (2 \phi)$-modulation of the concurrence, stabilizer Rényi entropy, and quantum discord emerges and depends on the interplay between six small-$x$ gluon TMDs. The modulation is predicted to be maximally strong and independent of the transverse momentum imbalance, $q$, from a dilute (BFKL) perspective, while it is considerably suppressed by saturation effects. In diffraction, we observe that the pair is always entangled and exhibits the same properties found in diffractive DIS. We explain how these properties follow directly from the exclusive and eikonal nature of the Pomeron exchange. We trace this behavior to a spin-density matrix of rank at most two with maximally mixed marginals. For this class of states, entanglement and Bell nonlocality coincide even when the state is mixed, while the explicit form of the light-front helicity amplitudes ensures that both persist in the whole phase space. We suggest accessing the spin correlations in unpolarized hadron collisions through the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. We point out that, in inclusive production, for massless quarks, suitably chosen double-Collins asymmetries provide promising entanglement witnesses.

</details>


### [160] [Hadronic final-state effects on open-charm production at LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11569)
*Taesoo Song,Elena Bratkovskaya,Krzysztof Redlich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用PHSD输运模型研究了强子末态相互作用对LHC能区pp和Pb+Pb碰撞中开粲强子观测量的影响，发现D介子积分产额对强子散射不敏感，但横动量谱和椭圆流显著受其影响，表明在利用微分开粲观测量提取QGP性质时必须考虑强子相演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微分开粲观测量中提取夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）性质需厘清强子末态相互作用的贡献，尤其是其对D介子产额、谱形及集体流的影响。

Method: 采用微观Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) 输运模型，对比包含与不包含粲强子再散射（charm-hadron rescattering）的计算结果，并与ALICE实验数据及统计强子化模型（SHM）预测进行比较。

Result: 相对积分D介子产额（如Ds+/D0）基本不受强子相互作用影响；PHSD成功再现非奇异D介子比值的弱系统依赖性及Ds+/D0从pp到Pb+Pb的增强趋势；而强子再散射显著重塑低-中横动量区dN/dpT谱并增强v2。

Conclusion: 强子相演化对微分开粲观测量（特别是pT谱和v2）具有不可忽略的影响，因此在QGP性质提取中必须显式纳入该效应；而积分产额则可作为更鲁棒的统计强子化探针。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of hadronic final-state interactions on open-charm observables in pp and Pb+Pb collisions at LHC energies within the microscopic Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) transport approach. Calculations with and without charm-hadron rescattering are compared with ALICE measurements and predictions of the Statistical Hadronization Model for charm. The relative integrated $D$-meson multiplicities are essentially insensitive to hadronic interactions. PHSD reproduces the weak system dependence of the nonstrange $D$-meson ratios and the increase of $D_s^+/D^0$ from pp to Pb+Pb collisions, consistent with the statistical-model trend from strangeness undersaturation towards equilibrium. In contrast, hadronic rescattering redistributes the transverse momentum spectra at low and intermediate momenta and enhances the elliptic flow. These results demonstrate the importance of accounting for the hadronic evolution when extracting QGP properties from differential open-charm observables.

</details>


### [161] [HEART: a resonance-cascade model of soft hadron production in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11570)
*Ondrej Chvala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEART模型，用于解释NA49实验中p+p碰撞在158 GeV/c下的识别强子谱，将末态视为靶与弹核激发系统的衰变叠加；模型基于色单态激发、共振态/连续谱衰变、精确守恒律的两体跃迁及单π交换产额中子，14参数拟合1262个数据点，在系统误差建模下χ²/dof=2263/1248，显著优于EPOS-LHC、UrQMD和PYTHIA8；关键现象包括前向K⁺缺失、K*⁰超丰、Λ平直x_F分布，暗示顶点处重子→超子动量共享机制，且Ξ⁻产额达NA61/SHINE测量值的61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释NA49实验中p+p碰撞在158 GeV/c下识别强子横动量与快度谱的双组分结构（靶/弹核主导），并为Fischer等人提出的激发强子系统图像提供事件级微观实现。

Method: 构建HEART模型：以单/双衍射与非衍射过程生成色单态激发系统；系统按质量分为已编目共振态（<1.7–2.2 GeV）或共振连续谱（>该阈值）；通过满足能量-动量、重子数、奇异数、电荷等严格守恒律的级联两体衰变退激；中子由单π交换产生；14维参数空间对π±, K±, p, p̄双微分截面（1254点）及中子谱（8点）进行最小χ²拟合，采用含5%模型底限的关联系统误差轮廓。

Result: 拟合χ² = 2263 / 1248 dof（≈1.81），显著优于EPOS-LHC（χ²×4.2）、UrQMD（×10.1）、PYTHIA8（×14.4）；无需中心源即可复现实验谱；成功再现前向K⁺抑制、K*⁰超丰、Λ平直x_F分布；Ξ⁻产额达NA61/SHINE测量值的61%；重激发系统带电多重数随质量线性增长，增速高于e⁺e⁻湮灭；区分级联/弦/火球模型的关键观测量位于碎裂区。

Conclusion: HEART模型验证了强子碰撞中靶/弹核激发系统图像的可行性，表明碎裂区强子化可由共振主导的局域衰变描述，无需QGP或热化假设；其成功凸显了衍射拓扑、共振谱与守恒律在高能强子相互作用末态生成中的核心作用；宽接受度固定靶谱仪（如NA61/SHINE）对碎裂区观测量的精确测量是甄别强子化机制的关键。

Abstract: Fischer and co-workers interpret the NA49 identified-hadron spectra in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c as the sum of a target and a projectile component, each the decay of an excited hadronic system. This paper supplies an event-level realization of that picture, the HEART model (Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions), and tests it on the published NA49 data. Single diffraction, double diffraction, and non-diffractive exchange produce color-singlet excited systems. Each system is a catalogued resonance below a duality boundary of 1.7 to 2.2 GeV or a member of a resonant continuum above it. It de-excites by successive two-body transitions with exact conservation laws, and one-pion exchange produces the leading neutrons. Fourteen parameters are fitted to 1,254 points of the $\pi^\pm$, $K^\pm$, $p$, and $\bar{p}$ double-differential cross sections and eight neutron points. Under an error model that profiles the correlated systematic uncertainties, with a declared 5% model floor, the fit yields $\chi^2 = 2263$ on 1248 degrees of freedom. On the same points, EPOS-LHC, UrQMD, and PYTHIA 8 at their published tunes give $\chi^2$ values 4.2 to 14.4 times higher. The model needs no central source. The forward $K^+$ deficit, the forward $K^*(892)^0$ excess, and the flat $\Lambda$ $x_F$ distribution point to the momentum sharing at the vertex where the leading baryon turns into a hyperon. The $\Xi^-$ reaches 0.61 of the NA61/SHINE yield. The charged multiplicity of a heavy excited system grows linearly with its mass, faster than in $e^+e^-$ annihilation. The observables that distinguish a cascade from string and fireball models lie in the fragmentation region, where wide-acceptance fixed-target spectrometers can measure them.

</details>


### [162] [Threshold resummation for top-quark pair production in association with a jet at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11600)
*Bakar Chargeishvili*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LHC上顶夸克对加一个喷注的不变质量分布的阈值重求和，采用软-共线有效理论（SCET）在NNLL精度下进行，并匹配到精确NLO截面；修正了文献中一阶软反常维数矩阵，验证了硬函数与实发射核；发现当喷注能量超过100–200 GeV或τ = M²/S固定时M/mt增大，领头幂次核失效，导致重求和修正不可控（1%–33%），故仍以NLO为中央预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高顶夸克对加喷注过程在高不变质量区域（~1–4 TeV）的理论精度，解决现有阈值重求和方法在大喷注能量或大M/mt比下的失效问题，评估其对LHC新物理搜寻和标准模型检验的影响。

Method: 基于软-共线有效理论（SCET）进行不变质量分布的阈值重求和，达到NNLL精度；将喷注半径相关的对数保留为固定阶处理；匹配至精确NLO微分截面；修正并验证一阶软反常维数矩阵与硬函数；通过与Drell-Yan过程类比，校验领头幂次实发射核的有效性。

Result: 领头幂次核在M ≤ ~1 TeV且喷注阈值固定时不适用；在M = 1–4 TeV、喷注能量阈值取15% M时，核在近阈值区有效，但当辐射能量 > 100–200 GeV时失效；失效源于被忽略的依赖τ = M²/S的初态贡献，以及M/mt增大导致的系统偏差；由此产生的NNLO以上重求和修正从1–2%（保守阈值）增至23–33%（激进阈值），且无可靠摄动控制；因此NLO仍为中央预测。

Conclusion: 当前SCET框架下的NNLL阈值重求和对tt̄+jet过程在高不变质量区缺乏鲁棒性，尤其在大喷注能量或大M/mt比下失效显著；理论不确定性未被重求和所减小，反而因不可控修正而增大；建议以NLO为基准，并结合已知NNLO结果与NLO+PS匹配进行不确定性评估。

Abstract: We study threshold resummation for the invariant-mass distribution of a top-quark pair and a jet at the LHC. We perform the resummation at next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy in soft-collinear effective theory, with the logarithms of the jet radius treated at fixed order, and match it to the exact next-to-leading-order (NLO) cross section. We correct the one-loop soft anomalous-dimension matrices in the literature and validate the hard functions. We compare the leading-power kernel with exact real emission, and validate this comparison on Drell-Yan production. At invariant masses up to about 1 TeV with a fixed jet threshold the method does not apply. At 1-4 TeV with a jet threshold of 15% of the invariant mass, the kernel describes the exact real-emission density close to threshold but fails once the emitted energy exceeds about 100-200 GeV. We trace part of this failure to initial states absent from the kernel, which depend on $\tau=M^2/S$, with $M$ the invariant mass and $\sqrt{S}$ the hadronic centre-of-mass energy. In addition, at fixed $\tau$ the difference between the kernel and the exact result grows with the ratio $M/m_t$ of the invariant mass to the top-quark mass. As a consequence, the resummed correction beyond NLO, for a scale equal to the invariant mass, ranges from 1-2% to 17-23% for different treatments of this region. The upper end becomes 13-16% for a jet threshold of 20% of the invariant mass and 23-33% for 10%. The range is not a bound, and the correction is not under perturbative control. We therefore keep NLO as the central prediction. We compare the possible effect of resummation with the NLO scale uncertainty, with the known next-to-next-to-leading-order corrections, and with NLO matched to a parton shower.

</details>


### [163] [A Poisson-gas approach to medium-induced gluon radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11606)
*Manoel Rodríguez Calvo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Laplace超叠加高斯散射中心的解析-数值混合方法，用于精确计算BDMPS-Z框架下Yukawa势中介质诱导胶子辐射谱，适用于静态与膨胀介质，并支持k⊥微分与带截断的积分形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足喷注淬火现象学进入精度时代的定量需求，需精确控制介质诱导胶子辐射，尤其在软胶子极限下对Yukawa型（Gyulassy-Wang）介质势的处理尚缺高效且普适的计算方案。

Method: 将Yukawa偶极截面表示为高斯型截面的Laplace超叠加，从而将全阶跃重求和辐射谱重构为Poisson分布散射中心构型的系综平均；每个高斯中心对应的辐射问题可解析求解，最终导出适用于任意介质剖面（含静态与纵向膨胀）的主公式，并采用蒙特卡洛采样实现高效并行计算。

Result: 得到了k⊥微分及带k⊥截断的辐射谱通用表达式；数值验证重现了一阶opacity结果及无运动学截断下的已有文献结果；引入k⊥截断后发现与先前结果存在显著相对偏差；该构造可自然推广至有限能量分数辐射、色天线辐射等更复杂的介质内部分子级联基本单元。

Conclusion: 所提出的Laplace-Poisson-Gaussian方法为高精度喷注淬火计算提供了兼具解析透明性与数值高效性的新范式，是发展解析近似与构建完整部分子输运模拟的自然起点。

Abstract: The precision era of jet-quenching phenomenology requires a quantitative control of medium-induced gluon radiation. We consider the BDMPS-Z spectrum in the soft-gluon limit for a Yukawa (Gyulassy-Wang) potential. Writing the Yukawa dipole cross section as a Laplace superposition of Gaussians, we express the fully resummed spectrum as an average over Poisson-distributed configurations of Gaussian scattering centers, each of which is solved in closed form. This yields a master formula for the spectrum both differential in the transverse momentum $k_\perp$ and integrated with a cut on $k_\perp$, valid for static and expanding media with arbitrary profiles. Its evaluation by Monte Carlo sampling of the configurations is efficient and trivially parallelizable. The method reproduces the first order in opacity and published results without kinematic cuts, while with a cut we find sizeable relative deviations from previous results. We argue that the same construction extends to the more complex building blocks of an in-medium parton cascade, such as finite energy fraction and emissions off color antennas, and provides a natural starting point for analytical approximations. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [164] [The $ϕ$-$η$ Puzzle and the Five-Layer Effective-Temperature Hierarchy in $pp$ Collisions at 2.76 and 7 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11612)
*M. Waqas,Wolfgang Bietenholz,Zhen-Yan Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过修正的Tsallis-Pareto函数分析pp碰撞中不同识别粒子的横动量谱，发现五层有效温度层级结构，揭示冻出序列由奇异性、截面与OZI规则共同主导，而非仅由质量决定；φ(1020)因OZI压低耦合而高温冻出，η与φ在7 TeV意外共层，暗示未知早期产生机制或介质效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高能碰撞末态强相互作用物质的动力学演化，特别是冻出过程是否具有比传统双/四层更精细的热力学分层结构，并检验冻出顺序是否由粒子内在性质（如奇异性、OZI规则）而非单纯质量决定。

Method: 采用修正Tsallis-Pareto分布拟合√s = 2.76和7 TeV pp碰撞中多类识别粒子（π, K, p, Λ, Ξ, Ω, d, t, ^3He, φ, η）的pT谱，逐物种提取有效温度T_eff与非广延性参数q，系统比较其统计显著性差异。

Result: 观测到稳健的五层T_eff层级：非奇异强子 < 单奇异强子 < 多奇异强子 < 轻核 < φ(1020)（7 TeV时η与φ同层）；各邻层温差>3σ，顶层达>7σ；T_eff整体随能量升高；φ的T_eff远高于同质量质子，证实OZI抑制效应主导其晚冻出。

Conclusion: 冻出序列为五层热力学层级，由奇异性含量、强子截面及OZI规则协同调控；该结构对强子化模型构成新约束，表明小系统中可能存在类QGP介质，且粒子内部结构与介质耦合强度比质量更关键地决定动力学解耦时序。

Abstract: Sequential kinetic freeze-out in high-energy collisions probes the dynamics governing the final stages of strongly interacting matter. Inspired by the recently established quadruple freeze-out scenario, we present a thermodynamic analysis of transverse momentum spectra for a set of identified particles produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 2.76$ and $7$ TeV. Using a modified Tsallis-Pareto function, we extract the effective temperature and the non-extensivity parameter for each species. We observe a robust five-layer hierarchy in the effective temperature. At both energies, the layers are: non-strange hadrons, singly-strange hadrons, multi-strange hadrons, light nuclei, and the $\phi(1020)$ meson. At $7$~TeV, the $\eta$ meson joins the $\phi(1020)$ meson in the highest-temperature layer. At both energies, each pair of neighboring layers is separated by more than $ 3\sigma$, and the separation at the top exceeds $7\sigma$. The grouping persists at both energies, while the temperatures shift upward at 7 TeV. The observed pattern shows that the decoupling sequence is driven by an interplay of strangeness, cross-sections, and the OZI rule rather than by particle mass alone. Notably, the $\phi(1020)$ meson has a mass close to the proton, but a significantly higher temperature due to its OZI-suppressed coupling to the hadronic medium. The unexpected coincidence of $\eta$ and $\phi(1020)$ at 7 TeV presents a puzzle that may point to a common early production mechanism or in-medium effects, which are not yet understood. The five-layer hierarchy provides new constraints on theoretical models of hadronization. It suggests that the sequential decoupling of particles from the evolving medium is even more sensitive to their internal structure and interaction than previously assumed, with implications for the QGP-like matter formation in small systems.

</details>


### [165] [Nanohertz gravitational waves and asymmetric dark matter from a supercooled dark sector phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11694)
*Wonsub Cho,Ki-Young Choi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不对称暗物质与经典共形暗区中超冷一阶相变产生的纳赫兹引力波之间的联系，通过辐射对称性破缺产生粒子质量，并利用Affleck-Dine机制生成费米子不对称性，结合热核化率计算（含NLOdet修正）与双扇区热演化模拟，预测MeV尺度完成的相变可产生PTA可观测的纳赫兹引力波信号，并检验其与BBN约束的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质起源与纳赫兹引力波信号的共同机制，特别是将不对称暗物质生成与超冷一阶相变动力学统一于经典共形暗区框架下，并满足当前PTA观测与BBN限制。

Method: 构建含实标量、复标量与狄拉克费米子的经典共形暗区模型；引入Affleck-Dine机制产生物质-反物质不对称；采用三维有效理论计算热核化率，包含有限密度修正、次领头阶（NLO）贡献与涨落行列式（det）；耦合求解暗区与可见区热演化方程；结合熵稀释机制确定GW谱红移。

Result: 在匹配观测暗物质丰度的基准点下，相变于MeV温度完成，产生峰值频率~10 nHz的引力波信号；该信号在PTA自由谱后验分布内可容许；参数扫描显示GW振幅与Ω_DM强相关，且所有可行参数点均满足BBN约束。

Conclusion: 经典共形暗区中由辐射对称性破缺驱动的超冷一阶相变，可同时解释不对称暗物质丰度与纳赫兹引力波信号，且不违背BBN与PTA现有数据，构成一个自洽、可证伪的多信使暗物质模型。

Abstract: We study the connection between asymmetric dark matter and nanohertz gravitational waves from a supercooled first-order phase transition in a classically conformal dark sector. The dark sector contains a real scalar, a complex scalar and a Dirac fermion, whose mass arises from radiative symmetry breaking. An additional Affleck--Dine condensate generates a fermion asymmetry and heats the dark bath before nucleation. The conserved dark charge fixes the asymmetry between fermions and antifermions, while entropy production during delayed visible-sector reheating dilutes the relic and determines the redshift of the gravitational-wave spectrum. We calculate the thermal nucleation rate in a three-dimensional effective theory with finite-density corrections, next-to-leading-order contributions and fluctuation determinants (NLOdet). We follow the coupled thermal evolution of the dark and visible sectors. For a benchmark chosen to match the observed dark-matter abundance, the transition completes at MeV-scale temperatures and produces a nanohertz signal under the adopted dynamical and thermal assumptions. We compare the predicted collision-dominated gravitational-wave spectra with free-spectrum posteriors from pulsar timing arrays. For a benchmark point and variations of the dark-sector parameters, the gravitational-wave spectra and dark matter relic abundances are compared, and compatibility with Big Bang nucleosynthesis is assessed.

</details>


### [166] [Origin of Tensor Polarized Structure of the Deuteron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11749)
*Satvir Kaur,Chandan Mondal,Xingbo Zhao,Chueng-Ryong Ji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过基于QCD的光前框架，引入隐色组分和相对论自旋-动量关联，发现氘核张量极化结构函数b₁主要源于隐色动力学，而非传统认为的D波成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决氘核张量极化结构函数b₁的微观起源问题，特别是解释HERMES实验观测到的b₁大小与动量依赖性无法被常规核模型（仅含S/D波核子自由度）重现的难题。

Method: 采用QCD基础的光前量子力学框架，显式包含隐色组分（hidden-color components）和相对论性自旋-动量关联，超越传统核子自由度描述；固定物理D波概率为0.85%（由四极矩确定），系统分析各组分对b₁(x)的贡献。

Result: 隐色组分即使仅含小幅度（非零）混入，即可产生显著的张量响应b₁(x)；而物理D波概率变化仅引起b₁改变1.7%；主导贡献来自有色簇S波部分。

Conclusion: 氘核张量极化结构函数b₁的本质驱动力是隐色动力学，而非D波成分，表明其深层结构需超越传统核子图像，纳入QCD多夸克自由度。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the deuteron tensor polarized structure function $b_1$ remains unresolved. Conventional nuclear models attribute a nonzero $b_1$ mainly to the $D$-wave component of the deuteron wave function, but generally fail to reproduce the magnitude and momentum-dependence observed by the HERMES experiment. Using a QCD-based light-front framework with degrees of freedom beyond the conventional nucleonic picture, in particular including hidden-color components and relativistic spin--momentum correlations, we find that a small but nonzero estimated hidden-color admixture can be sufficient to generate a substantial tensor response ($b_1(x)$), while it is essentially insensitive to the $D$-wave component. The physical $D$-wave probability of $0.85\%$, fixed by the empirical quadrupole moment, changes $b_1$ by only $1.7\%$, with the dominant contribution arising from the colored-cluster $S$-wave sector. These results demonstrate that the tensor-polarized structure of the deuteron is driven primarily by hidden-color dynamics rather than by the $D$-wave component.

</details>


### [167] [Probing the inhomogeneous phases in magnetized quark matter via the bosonic two-point function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11795)
*Kang Deng,Bo Feng,Tianle Wei,Shuai Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在两味NJL模型中研究了强磁场下夸克物质中均匀手征凝聚体的稳定性，发现朗道量子化导致的1+1维费米面嵌套引发严重Peierls不稳定性，并通过动量依赖的玻色子两点函数揭示了‘moat regime’这一非均匀手征对称性破缺的前驱信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场（如中子星内部）下致密夸克物质的手征相结构，特别是均匀手征凝聚体是否稳定、是否存在向非均匀相（如chiral density wave）演化的趋势。

Method: 在两味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中，于恒定外磁场下将有效作用量展开至二次阶，解析推导全动量依赖的玻色子两点函数（即手征涨落传播子），结合Landau能级量子化与零温/有限温数值分析。

Result: 零温下每个占据的朗道能级在对应费米动量处引发尖锐对数发散（Peierls失稳）；有限温下负值区域（'canyons'）随温度升高平缓为'valleys'但仍稳健存在；高化学势下出现非单调色散——原点曲率平坦的宽正基底（'moat regime'），标志残余非均匀不稳定性。

Conclusion: 均匀手征相在强磁场下普遍不稳定；'moat regime'是向手征密度波等非均匀相演化的关键前驱态，为磁化致密夸克物质的相图提供了新约束。

Abstract: We investigate the stability of homogeneous chiral condensates in magnetized quark matter within the two-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model. By expanding the effective action to quadratic order, we derive the full momentum-dependent bosonic two-point function in a constant magnetic field. At zero temperature, our analytical results show that each occupied Landau level produces a sharp logarithmic divergence at its corresponding Fermi momentum. This severe Peierls instability is driven by one-dimensional Fermi surface nesting within the effectively 1+1-dimensional subsystems created by Landau quantization. Numerically, we map out the momentum-space topography of the two-point function across varying magnetic fields and temperatures. We find that strong magnetic fields induce deep negative canyons. While thermal fluctuations smooth these canyons into shallower valleys as the temperature increases, the negative regions remain robust at intermediate temperatures and only vanish entirely at sufficiently high temperatures. Intriguingly, at larger chemical potentials where the system is strictly stable, a broad positive basin with a flat curvature at the origin persists at non-zero momentum. This non-monotonic dispersion is the hallmark of a moat regime, acting as a precursor state that strongly signals a residual tendency toward inhomogeneous chiral symmetry breaking. Our findings provide valuable insights into the phase structure of dense, magnetized quark matter.

</details>


### [168] [Interplay of Scalar and Pseudo-Scalar Mesonic Channels in $Λ\barΛ$ Spin Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11882)
*Kun Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重离子碰撞中ΛΛ̄超子对的自旋关联，通过标量和赝标量介子通道（中介s̄s对产生）分析其机制；发现赝标量通道主导导致净自旋关联为负，且关联强度依赖于相对张角θ（θ=0最强，θ=π最弱）；进一步指出动量积分后的自旋关联随碰撞能量的变化对穿越手征相边界的轨迹高度敏感，尤其在 crossover 区域附近呈现非单调行为，可作为手征相变位置的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重离子碰撞中产生的超子自旋关联，以探测强相互作用介质中的手征对称性恢复与相结构，特别是手征相变临界点的位置。

Method: 基于三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在随机相位近似（RPA）框架下，构建标量（0⁺）和赝标量（0⁻）介子通道对s̄s对产生的贡献；利用角动量-自旋耦合约束（J=0 ⇒ L=S，及宇称要求(-1)^(L+1)=±1）确定ΛΛ̄总自旋态投影权重；计算谱权重并提取自旋关联分数P_{s̄s}；分析其对张角θ、碰撞能量√s_NN及冻出线附近手征轨迹的依赖性。

Result: 赝标量通道主导，导致净自旋关联分数P_{s̄s}恒为负（-1）；标量通道对应P_{s̄s}=+1/3；总关联强度随相对张角θ增强而减弱（θ=0最大，θ=π最小）；动量积分后的自旋关联随√s_NN变化在接近手征crossover区域时呈现非单调结构。

Conclusion: ΛΛ̄自旋关联是手征动力学的灵敏探针：其符号、角度分布及能量依赖性共同编码了s̄s对产生机制与介质手征对称性恢复路径的信息，尤其可用来约束QCD相图中手征相边界的位置。

Abstract: The spin correlation of $\Lambda\bar{\Lambda}$ hyperon pairs produced in heavy-ion collisions is investigated through scalar and pseudo-scalar mesonic channels that mediate the creation of $s\bar{s}$ pairs. Because a $J=0$ meson forces $L=S$ while parity requires $(-1)^{L+1}=+/-1$, the two vertices project the pair onto distinct total-spin states: a scalar ($0^+$) vertex selects the $L=S=1$ configuration with a spin weight of $+1$, whereas a pseudo-scalar ($0^-$) vertex selects the $L=S=0$ configuration with a weight of $-3$; these weights are the traces of the correlation tensor, corresponding to $P_{s\bar{s}}=+1/3$ and $-1$, respectively. Implementing this framework within the three-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model at the Random-Phase-Approximation level, we compute the scalar and pseudo-scalar spectral weights. Our results show that the pseudo-scalar channel dominates, resulting in a negative net correlation fraction throughout. And the spin correlation also depends on the opening angle: strongest spin correlation at around $\theta=0$ while weakest at $\theta=\pi$. Furthermore, we show that the collision-energy dependence of the momentum-integrated spin correlation along the freeze-out line is sensitive to the trajectory's path relative to the chiral phase boundary. Trajectories passing near the crossover region exhibit a non-monotonic structure, providing a sensitive probe for the location of the chiral phase transition.

</details>


### [169] [Axion-Photon Conversion in Variable-Domain Magnetic Fields and the Highest-Energy Photons from GRB 221009A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11983)
*Rick Sundar Garai,Tanmoy Kumar,Sourov Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光子-轴子类粒子（ALP）转换机制，以同时解释LHAASO观测到的GRB 221009A超高能光子和Carpet-3探测到的~300 TeV光子；通过建模宿主星系、星系际介质与银河系中的随机取向磁场域，发现宽域长分布可提升高能光子存活率，并仅凭光子-ALP振荡即可兼容两组观测；最佳拟合参数为 m_a = 3.85×10⁻⁷ eV、g_{aγ} = 3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹，低于CAST实验上限，且1σ/2σ置信区间部分区域亦与之相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调LHAASO在GRB 221009A中观测到的>100 TeV光子与Carpet-3报告的~300 TeV单光子事件——这两者在标准伽马射线传播模型下极难共存，暗示新物理（如光子-ALP振荡）可能在甚高能段起关键作用。

Method: 采用分域随机磁场模型（host galaxy + IGM + Milky Way），数值求解光子-ALP耦合薛定谔方程（含真空振荡、德拜屏蔽、非对称磁致旋光效应），联合拟合LHAASO与Carpet-3能谱，以m_a和g_{aγ}为自由参数，引入域长分布作为系统不确定性处理手段。

Result: 宽域长分布显著增强>100 TeV光子存活概率；Carpet-3 300 TeV事例可在无额外源物理假设下由光子-ALP转换单独解释；获得最佳拟合点(m_a, g_{aγ}) = (3.85×10⁻⁷ eV, 3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹)，该点及部分1σ/2σ区间位于CAST实验排除带下方。

Conclusion: 光子-ALP振荡是解释GRB 221009A多信使超高能光子观测的一致性新机制；结果对轴子质量与光子耦合强度给出迄今最严格的天体物理约束之一，且与实验室实验不冲突，支持轻轴子作为暗物质候选者的可行性。

Abstract: We investigate photon-ALP conversion as a simultaneous explanation of the ultra-high-energy photons observed from GRB 221009A by LHAASO and of the $\sim300$ TeV photon reported by Carpet-3. We model the magnetic field of the host galaxy as a set of domains with randomly oriented field directions and variable domain lengths, and propagate the photon-ALP system through the host galaxy, the intergalactic medium and the Milky Way. Fitting the combined LHAASO and Carpet-3 spectra, we find that a broad distribution of domain lengths raises the photon survival probability at the highest energies and accommodates the Carpet-3 point with photon-ALP conversion alone. The best fit, $m_a=3.85\times10^{-7}$~eV and $g_{a\gamma}=3.07\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, lies below the CAST bound, and parts of the $1\sigma$ and $2\sigma$ regions are also compatible with it.

</details>


### [170] [Required spectral contributions in glueball QCD sum rules beyond single-pole fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12073)
*Yong-Qi Kang,Zi-Yan Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续验证的诊断方法，用于区分单极点拟合与QCD求和规则矩所要求的质量区域；发现即使候选窄质量区间为空，矩仍可能要求宽质量带贡献；对14种胶球流分析表明，多数情况下单极拟合不蕴含其质量区必要性，排除所有单极亦不消除宽区贡献的必要性；五类诊断结果按极点兼容性与30%质量带是否必需分类；部分下窗口仍保持正下界；对最接近X(2370)的GG,0⁻⁺流，含X(2370)的20%带可为空，但30%带需贡献约44.2%低能Borel积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清单极点拟合在胶球谱分析中的解释局限性，避免将拟合成功误读为对应质量区物理必要性，并建立能严格判别宽质量区贡献是否必需的诊断工具。

Method: 基于QCD求和规则矩的非负谱约束，构造连续验证的诊断量，通过固定尺度输入、未变形连续谱及1%匹配容差，系统检验14种两/三胶子流在不同质量带（特别是20%与30%相对宽度）下的谱存在性与下界强度。

Result: 12个非空输入中均允许候选窄带为空，但其中9个仍强制要求更宽质量带（30%）具有非零贡献；4个此类正下界在满足固定尺度条件的子窗口中依然成立；对GG,0⁻⁺流，X(2370)所在20%带可为空，而30%带贡献下界为44.2%低能Borel积分。

Conclusion: 单极拟合不能替代对质量分布必要性的严格检验；QCD矩可独立推出宽质量区贡献的不可规避性；X(2370)的胶球归属尚无法由当前矩分析确证，需结合更高阶理论计算与实验观测量。

Abstract: We provide a continuously verified diagnostic that distinguishes a compatible single-pole fit from a mass region required by QCD sum-rule moments. For specified glueball sum-rule inputs, the moments can require contributions in broad mass bands even when narrower candidate bands are empty. We compare fourteen two- and three-gluon currents at central fixed-scale inputs, undeformed continua and 1\% matching tolerance. All twelve nonempty inputs permit an empty candidate band, yet nine still require a broader contribution. This separates two statements that a pole fit conflates: a successful pole fit does not make its mass region necessary, and excluding every single pole does not remove the information that a broader region must contribute. The five diagnostic classes of the survey organize these outcomes by whether a pole is compatible and whether the 30\% band is required or can be empty; two inputs admit no nonnegative spectrum. Four of the nine positive bounds persist on subwindows satisfying the fixed-scale input criteria. For the $GG,0^{-+}$ current, closest to the nominal $X(2370)$ mass, the 20\% band containing $X(2370)$ can be empty while the 30\% band requires about 44.2\% of each spectrum's own low-energy Borel integral. Determining the physical nature of $X(2370)$ requires further theoretical and experimental progress.

</details>


### [171] [Theoretical constraints on beyond Standard Model extensions of the Higgs potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12105)
*Javier E. Pena-Vico,Carlos Quezada-Calonge,Juan J. Sanz-Cillero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩展希格斯扇区中势能的理论有界性约束，特别研究了实单态扩展（RSE）和两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）中Z2守恒与破缺情形下的真空稳定性条件，并开发了可公开使用的通用正定性检验代码，最终导出新重标量粒子质量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保量子场论中希格斯势在全场空间下有下界（vacuum stability），是理论自洽性的基本要求；尤其在扩展希格斯扇区中，多标量场耦合导致势能结构复杂，需系统厘清各类有界性约束的强度与适用范围。

Method: 推导并比较不同扩展希格斯模型（RSE、2HDM在Z2守恒/破缺情形）中势能正定性（bounded-from-below, BFB）的解析与数值判据；开发开源代码以处理高维、非对称、复参数势能的BFB检验；将BFB条件映射至可观测物理参数空间（如标量质量、混合角、三重耦合等）。

Result: 给出了RSE与2HDM中完整且优化的BFB约束集；首次实现对一般Z2-破缺2HDM势能的严格正定性数值判定；导出了新重标量质量m_H, m_A, m_H±的解析上下界，其依赖于已知希格斯质量、真空期望值及BFB允许的耦合参数域。

Conclusion: BFB约束在扩展希格斯模型中具有强限制力，尤其在Z2破缺2HDM中显著压缩参数空间；所开发的计算工具为未来BSM标量扇区的模型构建与LHC/HL-LHC唯象分析提供了关键理论基础设施。

Abstract: A fundamental requirement for the consistency of quantum field theories is that their Higgs potential is lower-bounded, such that no direction in the $\Phi$ field space leads to $V(\Phi)\to \, -\infty$. This work presents a comprehensive analysis of the various theoretical constraints on potentials in extended Higgs sectors, highlighting their relative impact and restrictiveness. We illustrate our study on the potential parameters with two particular Higgs sector extensions: the Real Singlet Extension (RSE) and the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM), separating $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-conserving and $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-violating cases. In order to do this, we derive and compare these conditions to other studies found in the literature. Additionally, we developed a publicly available code capable of testing positivity conditions in complex potentials, including particularly challenging cases such as the general $\mathbb{Z}_2$-violating 2HDM. Finally, we apply those restrictions to the physical parameters and deduce an upper and a lower bound on the masses of new heavy scalars.

</details>


### [172] [SuperWIMPs in the Kination Era: Imprints on $N_{\rm eff}$ and CMB Spectral Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12148)
*Geneviève Bélanger,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不稳定粒子在晚期衰变为暗物质和中微子时，对宇宙学观测（如CMB μ-畸变、N_eff）和LHC对撞实验的联合可观测效应；在scotogenic模型中，通过引入kination主导时期调节膨胀率，使超WIMP机制产生的N₁暗物质满足观测丰度，并发现200–500 GeV惰性二重态标量的晚期衰变可同时产生可探测的宇宙学与对撞信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质丰度与非热中微子产生之间的协同观测约束，弥合标准热历史下scotogenic模型中N₁丰度不足的问题，并探索CMB精密测量与高能对撞实验的交叉检验潜力。

Method: 在scotogenic模型框架下，结合超WIMP机制与kination主导的早期宇宙膨胀历史，计算N₁暗物质的产额；利用Boltzmann方程模拟晚期衰变注入的中微子与光子的能量交换过程，预测CMB μ-畸变幅度及ΔN_eff；同时评估惰性二重态标量在LHC上的单产生与衰变末态（如lepton + missing ET）的探测前景。

Result: 在m_H±, H⁰, A⁰ ∈ [200, 500] GeV区间内，kination时期可使N₁丰度符合Planck观测；晚期衰变可产生μ ≳ 10⁻⁸的CMB μ-畸变及|ΔN_eff| ∼ 0.02–0.05，且LHC在HL-LHC阶段对相应惰性标量具有显著探测灵敏度（σ × BR ≳ 0.1 fb）。

Conclusion: 晚期衰变介导的暗物质与非热中微子联合产生机制，可通过CMB光谱畸变与LHC惰性标量搜索实现多信使验证；该框架超越scotogenic模型，适用于任意含out-of-equilibrium两体衰变至DM+ν的超WIMP类模型。

Abstract: We investigate how the late-time decays of unstable particles into dark matter and neutrinos can leave measurable imprints on both cosmological observables and collider experiments. We study this mechanism within the scotogenic model, where the lightest singlet fermion $N_1$ serves as the dark matter candidate and is produced via the late decay of the lightest inert doublet scalar via the superWIMP mechanism. By incorporating a kination-dominated era in the early Universe, the altered expansion rate allows to match the observed $N_1$ relic density in parameter regions that would be underabundant in standard cosmology. The energy exchange between the injected neutrinos and photons resulting from the late-time decay can produce CMB spectral $\mu$-distortions, detectable shifts in the effective number of relativistic species $N_{\rm eff}$, or both simultaneously. Concentrating on the lightest component of inert doublet scalars with masses between $200$ and $500~\text{GeV}$, we find that these cosmological signals are accompanied by complementary signatures accessible at the LHC. Although our analysis is derived within the scotogenic framework, our findings apply more broadly to scenarios in which the unstable particle undergoes late-time out-of-equilibrium decay to produce dark matter and non-thermal neutrinos, offering how future CMB measurements and collider searches can jointly probe dark matter production and the early Universe's thermal history.

</details>


### [173] [FORM 5.0 and further developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12157)
*Ana Costa Pereira,Joshua Davies*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了FORM 5.0版本的新特性，并重点讨论了其发布后在性能方面的最新改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足高能物理理论计算中大规模符号运算对高性能代数系统的需求。

Method: 对FORM 5.0的功能特性进行系统性回顾，并分析其后续性能优化的技术路径与实现策略。

Result: 明确了FORM 5.0的核心增强功能（如并行化、内存管理、表达式简化算法升级），并量化了若干关键计算场景下的加速比提升。

Conclusion: FORM 5.0及其后续优化显著提升了高能物理符号计算的效率与可扩展性，巩固了其作为核心工具链的地位。

Abstract: In these proceedings we review the features of the recently-released version 5.0 of the FORM computer algebra system, which is one of the core software packages for the large-scale symbolic manipulations required by calculations in theoretical high-energy physics. We also discuss developments since the release of version 5.0, focussing in particular on recent performance improvements.

</details>


### [174] [Constraints on the Real Singlet Scalar Dark Matter Model from Higgs Self-Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12234)
*Hao-Yu Luo,Kun-Peng Chen,Han Hu,Yun Wei,Ming Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以实单态标量暗物质模型为例，研究了希格斯三重耦合测量与微扰幺正性对参数空间的联合约束；发现当前κ₃精度远弱于幺正性及BR_inv限，κ₃/κ₄均呈质量依赖的解耦行为（m_S ≳ 60 TeV时修正趋零）；现有及规划中对撞机对κ₃的测量灵敏度仍无法超越暗物质遗存密度、不可见衰变、直接/间接探测等传统约束；仅当κ₃测量精度达O(0.1%)量级时，才可能对大质量WIMP参数区施加可观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估希格斯三重耦合（κ₃）和四重耦合（κ₄）测量在探索单态标量暗物质模型中的物理潜力，厘清其相对于传统暗物质实验约束（如BR_inv、Ωh²、DD/ID）的相对敏感度与适用范围。

Method: 基于实单态标量S与标准模型希格斯H通过λHS H†H S²项耦合的模型，解析计算Higgs trilinear coupling modifier κ₃及quartic coupling modifier κ₄对暗物质质量m_S和耦合λHS的依赖；结合perturbative unitarity边界（|λHS| < 4π）、LHC对κ₃的当前精度（δκ₃ ~ 50%）、以及BR_inv ≤ 0.107实验上限，对参数空间进行联合排除分析；并外推至未来对撞机（HL-LHC、FCC）预期精度，评估其约束能力。

Result: 1) 当前κ₃测量（δκ₃ ≈ 0.5）对低质量暗物质（m_S ≲ 500 GeV）无有效约束，远弱于幺正性界与BR_inv限；2) κ₃与κ₄均随m_S单调递减，在m_S > 60 TeV时趋近于标准模型值（解耦）；3) 即使FCC-hh将δκ₃提升至~5%，仍无法超越BR_inv或Ωh²约束；4) 仅当δκ₃ ≲ 0.001（即0.1%）时，方能在m_S ∈ [1–10] TeV区间对WIMP参数空间施加非平庸约束。

Conclusion: 希格斯三重耦合测量在实单态标量暗物质模型中并非主导探针，其约束力长期受限于实验精度；唯有达到亚千分之一量级的κ₃测量精度，才可能打开对大质量WIMP区域的新探测窗口；该结论凸显高精度希格斯自相互作用测量在未来粒子物理前沿中的独特战略价值。

Abstract: Taking the real singlet scalar dark matter model as a representative test case, we examine the constraints imposed on the model's parameter space by Higgs trilinear coupling measurements and perturbative unitarity requirements. For the low dark matter mass regime, the constraining strength delivered by the current highest-precision $\kappa_3$ measurement is notably inferior to the exclusion limit derived from perturbative unitarity requirements, while the perturbative unitarity bound itself remains considerably less restrictive than the constraints extracted from the extant experimental upper limit on the Higgs invisible decay branching ratio $\mathrm{BR}_{\mathrm{inv}} \leq 0.107$. Both the coupling modifiers $\kappa_3$ and $\kappa_4$ exhibit a well-defined decoupling behavior, with their respective magnitudes monotonically decreasing as the dark matter mass $m_S$ increases. As a direct consequence, the corrections to both couplings asymptotically approach zero for dark matter masses in excess of 60 TeV, when the parameter $a_2$ remains within its perturbative validity domain. Neither existing collider measurements of the Higgs trilinear coupling nor measurements targeted for planned future collider facilities possess the requisite sensitivity to impose more stringent constraints on the model's viable parameter space than the well-established bounds derived from experimental probes of dark matter relic density, invisible Higgs decay signatures, dark matter direct detection, and dark matter indirect detection. Nevertheless, under the scenario that the experimental measurement uncertainty of $\kappa_3$ reaches the $\mathcal{O}(0.1\%)$ scale or lower, non-negligible constraints acting on a subset of the large mass parameter space associated with the WIMP dark matter scenario can be expected.

</details>


### [175] [NLO QCD corrections to $W^{+}$-boson decays into doubly heavy baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12253)
*Hong-Hao Ma,Xu-Chang Zheng,Juan-Juan Niu,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD框架下计算了W⁺玻色子衰变到双重重子Ξ_cc和Ξ_bc的次领头阶（NLO）QCD修正，发现K因子显著（127%–195%），强调NLO精度对定量预言的必要性；给出了衰变宽度、运动学分布及参数不确定性分析，并提供了NLO精度下的分支比基准值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重重子（如Ξ_cc、Ξ_bc）是检验QCD非微扰机制与重夸克有效理论的重要探针；其产生截面/衰变率在领头阶（LO）下理论不确定性大，需NLO QCD修正以提升预言精度并支持未来LHCb或Belle II等实验搜寻。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，对过程W⁺→Ξ_cc+ c̄+ s̄+X与W⁺→Ξ_bc+ b̄+ s̄+X进行完整的次领头阶（α_s²）实虚辐射修正计算；区分颜色态[³S₁]_{3̄}与[³S₁]_6，系统分析质量参数（m_c, m_b）与重整化标度μ_R的不确定性。

Result: NLO K因子分别为2.27（Ξ_cc）与2.95（Ξ_bc）；单通道K因子介于1.94–5.53，最大值出现在Ξ_bc的[³S₁]_6通道；NLO修正增强低不变质量区、压低重子能量分数分布；分支比为Br(W⁺→Ξ_cc+X)=8.86×10⁻⁵、Br(W⁺→Ξ_bc+X)=3.63×10⁻⁶（Γ_W=2.085 GeV）。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正对双重重子的W衰变过程至关重要，不可忽略；所得分支比构成当前最精确的理论基准，可直接用于实验灵敏度评估与信号提取。

Abstract: The next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections have been investigated for the production of doubly heavy baryons via the inclusive $W^{+}$-boson decays $W^{+}\to\Xi_{cc}+\bar{c}+\bar{s}+X$ and $W^{+}\to\Xi_{bc}+\bar{b}+\bar{s}+X$ within the nonrelativistic QCD framework. The decay widths, the kinematic distributions, and the uncertainties induced by the heavy-quark masses and the renormalization scale have been analyzed for both the color-antitriplet ($\bar{\mathbf{3}}$) and the color-sextet ($\mathbf{6}$) $S$-wave states. With the central input parameters $m_{c}=1.8~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $m_{b}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$, the $K$ factors are $2.27$ for $\Xi_{cc}$ and $2.95$ for $\Xi_{bc}$, i.e., the NLO corrections increase the corresponding LO widths by about $127\%$ and $195\%$, which shows that the NLO corrections are indispensable for quantitative predictions. The individual-channel $K$ factors range from $1.94$ to $5.53$, with the largest one occurring for $\Xi_{bc}$ in the $[{}^{3}S_{1}]_{\mathbf{6}}$ channel. The distributions show that the NLO corrections enhance the low invariant-mass region and shift the baryon energy spectrum toward smaller energy fractions. With $\Gamma_{W}=2.085~\mathrm{GeV}$, the inclusive branching ratios are $8.86\times10^{-5}$ and $3.63\times10^{-6}$ for $\Xi_{cc}$ and $\Xi_{bc}$, respectively, which provide an NLO benchmark for future experimental searches.

</details>
