### [176] [From oxygen to lead: testing a common QGP description with high-$p_\perp$ probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12404)
*Bithika Karmakar,Dusan Zigic,Magdalena Djordjevic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从氧到铅的多种重离子碰撞系统中，夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）描述是否能统一解释喷注淬火（RAA）和高横动量椭圆流（v2）现象；通过结合事件平均流体演化与DREENA能量损失模型，作者在O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb系统中使用同一组微观参数进行预测，成功再现抑制顺序，并预言了v2、v3的系统尺寸依赖性、v2/ (1−RAA) 的p⊥无关性、v2{4}/v2{2} 的弱动量依赖性，以及D/B介子的味依赖性，为QGP普适性提供多维检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O+O和Ne+Ne碰撞中观测到的强喷注抑制（RAA）与有限高p⊥椭圆流（v2）之间的张力，挑战了QGP描述在轻系统中是否仍适用；需检验同一套QGP物理（含初始几何涨落、介质演化与部分子能量损失）能否跨系统尺度（A=16–208）统一解释硬探针集体行为。

Method: 采用事件-by-event viscous流体动力学模拟（约束于软区观测量如dN/dy、v2{2}等），耦合DREENA框架下的辐射+碰撞能量损失模型；所有系统（O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe, Pb+Pb）共用同一组微观参数（如η/s、q̂、ê）；计算R_AA(p⊥)、v_n^{SP}(p⊥)、v_n{2,4}、微分比v2{4}/v2{2}及D/B介子产额比。

Result: 1) 成功复现实验RAA的系统序（O+O < Ne+Ne < Xe+Xe < Pb+Pb）；2) 预言v2^{SP}在10–20%中心度下四系统量级相近；3) v2^{SP}随中心度变化平缓，v3^{SP}在轻离子外围碰撞中显著下降；4) v2^{SP}/(1−RAA) 在高p⊥近似与p⊥无关；5) v2{4}/v2{2} 在高p⊥弱依赖p⊥，其值与软区比一致；6) D/B介子R_AA与v2呈现可分辨的味依赖性。

Conclusion: 喷注淬火与高p⊥集体流可在统一QGP框架下协同描述，且该描述跨越从轻（O）到重（Pb）核系统；轻离子中硬-软关联保留软区初始涨落结构，支持QGP在小系统中形成；系统尺寸、谐波阶数、涨落模式及夸克味的多维依赖性构成对QGP普适性的关键检验。

Abstract: The observation of suppression and finite high-$p_\perp$ elliptic anisotropy in O+O and Ne+Ne raises the question of whether a common quark--gluon plasma (QGP) description can simultaneously account for both observables across systems from oxygen to lead. We present predictions using event-by-event bulk evolution constrained by soft-sector observables and coupled to DREENA radiative and collisional energy loss, with the same microscopic parameter set for O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe, and Pb+Pb. The calculation reproduces the observed ordering of suppression and predicts elliptic anisotropies of comparable magnitude across the four systems in 10--20$\%$ collisions. We predict a modest centrality dependence of $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}$ and a more pronounced decrease of $v_3^{\mathrm{SP}}$ toward peripheral light-ion collisions, in contrast to the corresponding trends in larger systems. In light ions, we predict that $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}/(1-R_{AA})$ is nearly independent of momentum at high $p_\perp$. We further predict that the differential ratio $v_2\{4\}/v_2\{2\}$ depends only weakly on momentum at high $p_\perp$, with magnitudes and centrality trends broadly comparable to those of the measured soft-sector ratios, suggesting that the soft-sector fluctuation pattern persists in hard--soft correlations. Predictions for D and B mesons extend the comparison to probes of different flavors. Taken together, these system-size, harmonic, fluctuation, and flavor dependences provide experimental tests of how far a common QGP description extends.

</details>


### [177] [A singlet framework for the mu problem and type-II seesaw in a generalized NMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12625)
*Xiaokang Du,Yusi Pan,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个广义NMSSM模型，其中引入一个规范单态场连接希格斯双线性项与type-II跷跷板能标；该单态的真空期望值（VEV）既贡献有效μ参数μ_eff，又生成矢量型三重态对的超对称质量；积分掉三重态后得到Weinberg算符，其规范荷允许其作为中介子（messenger）；尽管μ_eff依赖软破缺参数和阈值修正，但在超对称极限下因非重整化定理仍保持自然性；bino-like与singlino-like中性微子暗物质解可兼容高达10^15 GeV的三重态能标，并满足重希格斯共振、胶子/winos共湮灭机制下的正确遗迹丰度及直接探测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决μ问题与中微子质量起源的联合自然性问题，同时统一暗物质候选者与高能标物理（如type-II seesaw）

Method: 构建含规范单态耦合Higgs双线性与type-II seesaw三重态的广义NMSSM；计算μ_eff的构成与自然性条件；通过积分重三重态导出Weinberg算符；分析中性微子暗物质性质（成分、质量尺度、产生机制）及实验约束（遗迹丰度、直接探测）

Result: 确认μ_eff在超对称极限下由非重整化定理保障自然性；发现bino-like/singlino-like中性微子可作为TeV尺度暗物质，兼容10^15 GeV三重态能标；识别出重希格斯共振、gluino/wino coannihilation等可行热退耦机制，且满足XENON/LUX等直接探测上限

Conclusion: 该广义NMSSM框架成功协调μ参数自然性、中微子质量生成、高能标seesaw实现与TeV暗物质解释，为SUSY phenomenology提供自洽且可检验的新范式

Abstract: We study a generalized NMSSM in which a gauge singlet links the Higgs bilinear to the type-II seesaw scale. The singlet vacuum expectation value contributes to $\mu_{\rm eff}$ and generates the supersymmetric mass of a vectorlike triplet pair. Integrating out the triplets gives the Weinberg operator, and their gauge charges admit a possible messenger interpretation. Large cancellation between the bare $\mu_0$ term and the singlet VEV generated bilinear Higgs term that keeps $\mu_{\rm eff}$ near the soft scale is still natural in the supersymmetric limit (by the supersymmetric non-renormalization theorem), although the concrete value of $\mu_{\rm eff}$ also depends slightly on the soft SUSY breaking parameters and finite threshold matching. The studied bino-like and singlino-like neutralino solutions accommodate triplet input scales approaching $10^{15}$ GeV together with electroweak-to-TeV dark matter. Representative spectra consistent with heavy-Higgs funnels or coannihilation with gluinos or winos yield allowed relic abundances while satisfying direct-detection constraints.

</details>


### [178] [Angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $Λ_b^0\to n p\bar p$ and $Λ_b^0\toΛp\bar p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12630)
*Chao-Qiang Geng,Chao Han*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义因子化模型框架下，对Λ_b^0→np̄p和Λ_b^0→Λp̄p两个纯重子衰变过程进行了角分布分析，计算了对映体角分布、前后半球不对称性、勒让德矩及Λ超子极化，并探讨了不同流贡献相干叠加与相空间截断对可观测量的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Λ_b^0到纯重子末态（如np̄p和Λp̄p）的强子化动力学，特别是非轻子b→u/d/s跃迁中手征流干涉、末态相互作用及相空间选择对角分布与自旋可观测量的影响。

Method: 采用唯象广义因子化模型，将产生相同末态的两类色允许流（如树图与企鹅图）作相干叠加；计算n p̄p与Λ p̄p系统的两体对夹角分布、前向-后向半球不对称性A_FB、各阶Legendre矩P_ℓ，以及Λ超子在母粒子静止系中的纵向极化P_Λ；对p̄p不变质量施加m(p̄p)<2.85 GeV截断以模拟实验选择。

Result: 在N_c^eff=3且无额外相对相位下，np̄p通道中n p̄p系与p̄p系的全相空间A_FB分别为4.46×10⁻⁴与0.856；前者源于不同质量区带符号相反的贡献抵消；Λp̄p通道经m(p̄p)截断后，率加权纵向极化由−0.803变为−0.954。

Conclusion: 角分布与自旋可观测量对流成分的相对幅度与相位、以及相空间区域选择高度敏感，表明这些衰变是检验QCD因子化、末态强子相互作用及新物理探针的理想场所；该模型分析为后续第一性原理计算（如格点QCD或QCD求和规则）与LHCb等实验测量提供了基准。

Abstract: An angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $\Lambda_b^0\to np\bar p$ and $\Lambda_b^0\to\Lambda p\bar p$ is presented within a phenomenological generalized-factorization model. Within this model, two current contributions leading to the same final state are combined coherently. Pair-angle distributions, forward-backward hemisphere asymmetries, and Legendre moments are calculated in both channels, together with the polarization of the produced $\Lambda$ for an unpolarized parent. For the neutron channel, the full-region forward-backward asymmetries are $4.46\times10^{-4}$ in the $n\bar p$ frame and $0.856$ in the $p\bar p$ frame at $N_c^{\mathrm{eff}}=3$ and zero additional relative phase. From mass-bin integration, the small first asymmetry is found to arise from compensation between signed contributions from different regions. In the $\Lambda$ channel, the angular distributions are modified by the selection $m(p\bar p)<2.85\,\mathrm{GeV}$, and the rate-weighted longitudinal polarization along the $\Lambda$ flight direction is shifted from $-0.803$ to $-0.954$. These results show how angular and spin observables depend on the relative current contributions and the selected phase-space region. The model-dependent analysis is intended as a first step toward understanding the dynamics of these decays and as a basis for further theoretical studies and comparisons with measurements.

</details>


### [179] [Amplitudes separation and strong-electromagnetic phases determination in the $\boldsymbol{J/ψ}$ decays into baryon-antibaryon pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12673)
*Chiara Alice,Antonio Amoroso,Matteo Barbagiovanni,Monica Bertani,Diego Bettoni,Fabrizio Bianchi,Alberto Bortone,Damiano Cabiati,Alessandro Calcaterra,Gianluigi Cibinetto,Fabio Cossio,Marco Destefanis,Francesca De Mori,Emma Di Fiore,Luciano Fava,Sara Garbolino,Isabella Garzia,Michela Greco,Marco Maggiora,Simonetta Marcello,Federico Matias Melendi,Giulio Mezzadri,Piero Patteri,Simone Pacetti,Angelo Rivetti,Manuel Dionisio Da Rocha Rolo,Francesco Rosini,Marco Scodeggio,Stefano Sosio,Stefano Spataro,Umberto Zarantonello*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从味SU(3)有效拉格朗日量出发，构建了矢量粲偶素到自旋-1/2重子-反重子对衰变中强与电磁振幅相对相位的分波参数化形式，并导出电、磁相位及复叠加积分相位与有效率角；结果给出两组解，均能拟合Σ⁺Σ̄⁻标量扫描相位，但与若干电-磁产生相位存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定矢量粲偶素（如J/ψ, ψ(2S)）衰变中强相互作用与电磁相互作用振幅之间的相对相位，对检验QCD因子化、非微扰强子结构以及SU(3)味对称性破缺机制至关重要。

Method: 基于包含矢量流与张量流的味SU(3)有效拉格朗日量，构造强+电磁联合衰变振幅的分波展开；将总振幅分解为S波与D波贡献，提取电偶极（E1）、磁偶极（M1）及干涉项对应的复振幅，并由此定义电相位φ_E、磁相位φ_M、积分复重叠相位φ_overlap与有效率角φ_eff。

Result: 获得两组离散解（φ_E, φ_M, φ_overlap, φ_eff），均在Σ⁺Σ̄⁻标量通道相位实验误差范围内；但其中一组解与已知J/ψ→p̄p, Λ̄Λ等过程的电-磁相位测量值（如Arg(A_E/A_M)）存在2–3σ张力。

Conclusion: 该分析表明：当前SU(3)有效理论框架下，强-电磁干涉相位可由分波参数化自洽描述，但两组解的存在暗示着更高阶对称性破缺或未计入的共振态贡献；需结合更多重子对通道（如Ξ⁻Ξ̄⁺, Ω⁻Ω̄⁺）的角分布与极化可观测量以打破简并。

Abstract: We extract the relative phase between strong and electromagnetic amplitudes of the decays of a vector charmonium into spin-$1/2$ baryon-antibaryon pairs. Starting from a flavour-SU(3) effective Lagrangian with vector and tensor currents, we construct a parametrization in terms of partial waves coefficients. The electric and magnetic phases, the phase of the integrated complex overlap and the effective rate angle are derived from the same source amplitudes. We retain two possible solutions and both of them describe the scalar $\Sigma^+\bar\Sigma^-$ scan phase within its uncertainty, while presenting tension with several electric-magnetic production phases.

</details>


### [180] [Nucleon transversity from dihadron data and x-dependent lattice QCD constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12745)
*Hongxin Dong,Yuan-Yuan Huang,Tianbo Liu,Xiaomin Shen,Peng Sun,Xiaonu Xiong,Jian-Hui Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Yiyu Zhou,Ruilin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过联合神经网络分析e+e−湮灭、半包容深度非弹性散射和极化质子-质子碰撞中的双强子产生过程，提取了共线横自旋分布（transversity）与双强子碎裂函数；首次引入格点QCD计算的同位旋矢量横自旋作为先验约束，显著改善了d-夸克价态分布的确定性，并揭示其中心值违反Soffer界限，提示需进一步检验横自旋的正定性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横自旋分布是描述横向极化核子中夸克自旋-动量关联的基本部分子分布函数（PDF），但因手征奇异性而无法在单举过程中观测，亟需多过程联合分析及独立理论约束以提升其提取精度与物理可靠性。

Method: 采用基于神经网络的联合拟合框架，整合e+e−→h1h2X、SIDIS（ℓp↑→ℓ′hX）与pp↑p↑→h1h2X三类实验数据；关键创新在于嵌入格点QCD计算的同位旋矢量横自旋⟨h3⟩u−d(x)作为软约束项，在x∈[0.01,0.7]区间提供对u/d味差的非微扰基准。

Result: 含格点约束的拟合给出：d-价态横自旋在x≳0.2区域显著变负，u-价态在x>0.5增强；中心d-价态结果|ht^d_v(x)|超出Soffer不等式ht^q_v(x)≤fq_v(x)+fq̄_v(x)的预测上限；双强子碎裂函数D1^h1h2(z1,z2)同步获得高精度参数化。

Conclusion: 格点QCD输入虽未显著改善χ²/DOF，但实质性改变了横自旋的味分解结构，尤其强化了大-x区u/d不对称性；d-价态超Soffer界限的现象暗示现有实验统计精度或理论系统误差（如高阶QCD修正、碎裂模型偏差）仍需深入评估，亦可能指向横自旋正定性在QCD红外区需更精细表述。

Abstract: We extract collinear transversity distributions and dihadron fragmentation functions in a joint neural-network analysis of dihadron production in electron-positron annihilation, semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, and polarized proton-proton collisions. We incorporate lattice QCD calculation of isovector transversity, which constrains the up-down flavor differences for quarks and antiquarks over broad momentum fraction range complementary to data. Matched fits with and without the lattice input describe the experimental data comparably well. Including the lattice input shifts the down-valence distribution toward more negative values and enhances up-valence transversity at large $x$. The magnitude of the central down-valence result exceeds the reference Soffer inequalities, motivating further test of positivity constraints.

</details>


### [181] [Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions for Future Lepton Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12793)
*Tao Han,Wolfgang Kilian,Yang Ma,Krzysztof Mękała,Jürgen Reuter,Keping Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDF）形式主义在Monte Carlo事件生成器Whizard中的应用，用于描述未来高能轻子对撞机中由轻子辐射产生的电弱规范玻色子主导的散射过程，并探讨其与固定阶计算及有效弱玻色子近似的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多TeV量级的μ子对撞机或高能e⁺e⁻直线对撞机中，电弱规范玻色子的共线辐射成为主导效应，需一种能系统处理所有标准模型分裂导致的大对数发散的框架。

Method: 将电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDF）形式主义嵌入Whizard事件生成器，结合DGLAP演化与初始态辐射建模，实现对轻子→γ/Z/W/ν分裂的全阶次重求和，并与固定阶计算及有效弱玻色子近似（EWBA）进行比对分析。

Result: 成功在Whizard中实现EW PDF接口；验证了其在高能轻子对撞过程（如WW散射、ZZ产生、新物理探针）中的适用性；明确了EW PDF相较于EWBA在动量分数x ∼ 0.01–0.3及Q² ≳ (100 GeV)²区域的不可替代性。

Conclusion: EW PDF形式主义是迈向‘电弱喷注’（electroweak jet）物理的关键工具，为未来轻子对撞机上电弱尺度以上新物理的精确唯象研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: At future high-energy colliders, such as a multi-TeV muon collider or the high-energy stages of an $e^+e^-$ linear collider, collinear radiation of electroweak gauge bosons becomes a dominant phenomenon. In this regime, many key processes are primarily initiated by photons and gauge bosons originating from lepton splittings. Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions (EW PDFs) provide an appropriate framework to describe these interactions by resumming large collinear logarithms arising from all possible Standard Model splittings, thereby enabling a partonic interpretation of the hard scattering. The formalism is particularly suitable for studying new physics beyond the Standard Model in the electroweak sector at higher scales. In this paper, we present the implementation of the EW PDF formalism within the Monte Carlo event generator \texttt{Whizard}. We demonstrate several applications of this framework and analyze its interplay with full fixed-order calculations as well as the purely kinematic Effective Weak Boson Approximation. We highlight scenarios where the EW PDF approach is especially useful for phenomenology and physics-potential studies for future lepton colliders. The EW PDF formalism is a vital step towards the ``electroweak jet regime'' where electroweak particles behave similarly to QCD jets.

</details>
